### [1] [Rigidity of multisections to the universal cubic plane curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14128)
*Jinwen Yao*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Farb提出的问题：在每条光滑平面三次曲线上能否连续选择n个不同点。证明了对于n=27（最小的未解决情况），这种选择在同伦意义下是唯一的；对于n=36k+18，则不存在这样的选择。这是Banerjee-Chen工作的延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Farb提出的关于光滑平面三次曲线上连续选择不同点的存在性与唯一性问题，特别是填补该问题中尚未解决的最小情形（n=27）。

Method: 作为Banerjee-Chen工作的延续，采用同伦论和代数几何方法，分析在光滑平面三次曲线上连续选择n个不同点的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 对于n=27，这种连续选择在同伦意义下是唯一的；对于n=36k+18形式的正整数，不存在这样的连续选择。

Conclusion: 部分回答了Farb问题，确定了n=27时选择的唯一性以及n=36k+18时选择的不存在性，但该问题的一般情况仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the following question of Farb: For which positive integers n is it possible to choose n distinct points continuously on every smooth cubic plane curve? This paper is a continuation of the work of Banerjee-Chen. We prove that such choice is unique up to homotopy for n=27 (smallest open case), and does not exist for n=36k+18.

</details>


### [2] [The Stable Adjunction in $\mathbb{A}^1$-Homotopy Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14411)
*Ajay Srinivasan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了动机稳定同伦理论中悬垂谱与零空间函子之间伴随的同伦单子性定理，通过验证arXiv:2607.12124中一般单子性定理的假设条件，并建立了六个预备引理及弱交换性定理，同时发展了一个关于单子代数的泛函框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一般单子性定理应用于动机稳定同伦理论，证明悬垂谱与零空间函子之间的伴随具有同伦单子性，为动机无限环空间的Operadic识别原理提供工具基础。

Method: （1）验证动机稳定同伦理论满足arXiv:2607.12124中一般单子性定理的假设条件；（2）证明六个关于单纯动机同伦理论的预备结果，关键技术是零空间函子与单纯动机谱实现之间的弱交换性定理；（3）建立关于op-lax单子映射下单子代数的一般泛函框架。

Result: 成功证明了动机稳定同伦理论中悬垂谱与零空间函子伴随的同伦单子性定理，并建立了相应的单子代数泛函框架。

Conclusion: 所得的同伦单子性结果及泛函框架为证明动机无限环空间的Operadic识别原理提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We prove a homotopical monadicity theorem for the adjunction between the suspension spectrum and zeroth space functors in motivic stable homotopy theory. Our proof verifies that motivic stable homotopy theory satisfies the hypotheses of the general monadicity theorem of arXiv:2607.12124. In the process, we demonstrate six preliminary results in simplicial motivic homotopy theory, the main technical ingredient being a weak commutativity theorem between the zeroth space functor and realization of simplicial motivic spectra. We also elaborate on a general framework relating monadic algebras under op-lax maps of monads. This monadic framework is used in the proof of the main results and may be of independent interest as well. These simplicial results, and the accompanying monadic framework, may provide tools toward a conjectured operadic recognition principle for motivic infinite loop spaces.

</details>


### [3] [Higher covering spaces in an $\infty$-topos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14773)
*Virgile Constantin*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在任意 $\infty$-意象 $\mathscr{E}$ 中建立了 $(n-1)$-截断映射（称为 $n$-覆盖映射）的系统理论，实现了 $n$-覆盖与基本 $n$-群作用之间的 $n$-范畴等价，并推广了经典覆盖空间理论到高维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受经典覆盖空间理论的启发，作者希望在一般 $\infty$-意象中系统发展高维覆盖空间理论，建立 $n$-覆盖映射与基本 $n$-群作用之间的分类对应关系。

Method: - 利用 $\infty$-群及其 $\infty$-作用的精细分析；- 使用单值宇宙（univalent universes）与内蕴 Yoneda 嵌入；- 在 $\infty$-意象中定义 $n$-覆盖映射，并建立 $n$-范畴等价；- 研究 deck 变换 $n$-群的结构，特别对正规 $n$-覆盖给出商描述。

Result: - 证明了 $n$-覆盖与基本 $n$-群 $\Pi_n(X,x)$ 在 $(n-1)$-截断对象上 $\infty$-作用之间的 $n$-范畴等价；- 对点连通 $n$-覆盖给出子-$n$-群分类；- 将 deck 变换 $n$-群 $\mathscr{D}\mathrm{eck}(p)$ 识别为 $F$ 上的 $\Pi_n(X,x)$-等变自等价；- 对正规 $n$-覆盖，deck $n$-群是 $\Pi_n(X,x)$ 的商，进而由 $\pi_n(X,x)$ 的正规子群分类；- 在 $n=1$ 且 $\mathscr{E}$ 为同伦型 $\infty$-范畴时恢复经典覆盖空间理论；- 在层/étale $\infty$-意象中外部 deck 群恢复基空间的上同调；在黏合 $\infty$-意象中恢复流形的 $1$-覆盖理论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 $\infty$-意象中高维覆盖空间的完整分类理论，统一并推广了经典覆盖空间理论，并在多种具体 $\infty$-意象（层、étale、黏合）中得到应用，展示了其广泛的理论价值。

Abstract: We develop a systematic theory of $(n-1)$-truncated maps, called $n$-covering maps, in a fixed $\infty$-topos $\mathscr{E}$, guided by the analogy with classical covering spaces. We prove an equivalence of $n$-categories between $n$-coverings over a pointed connected object $(X,x)$ and $\infty$-actions of the fundamental $n$-group $Π_n(X,x)$ on $(n-1)$-truncated objects, which restricts to a classification of pointed connected $n$-coverings in terms of sub-$n$-groups of $Π_n(X,x)$. We study the $n$-group of deck transformations $\mathscr{D}\mathrm{eck}(p)$, identifying it with $Π_n(X,x)$-equivariant autoequivalences of the fiber $F$. For normal $n$-coverings, it is further described as a quotient of $Π_n(X,x)$, yielding a classification of such coverings in terms of normal subgroups of $π_n(X,x)$. For an arbitrary $n$-covering, the deck $n$-group arises as a quotient of a suitable normalizer. Our approach relies on a careful study of $n$-groups and their $\infty$-actions, on the use of univalent universes, and on an internal Yoneda embedding. When $n=1$ and $\mathscr{E}$ is the $\infty$-category of homotopy types, our results recover the classical theory of covering spaces. We further illustrate the theory in sheaf and étale $\infty$-topoi, where the external deck group recovers cohomology of the base, and in cohesive $\infty$-topoi, where it recovers the $1$-covering theory of manifolds.

</details>

### [4] [Thin-Shell Wormholes from Entropy-Induced Black-Hole Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14130)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从修正的黑洞熵出发，构造对称的薄壳虫洞，将Darmois-Israel junction条件用熵导数表示，分析了多种熵形式（Bekenstein-Hawking、Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto、Rényi、Kaniadakis、对数、圈量子引力、指数）下的表面应力、能量条件、稳定性。结果表明：对称构造要求负表面能密度，常数压分支径向不稳定，而变Chaplygin壳层可以稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一框架，从修正黑洞熵的热力学途径研究薄壳虫洞，探索熵诱导有效时空几何对虫洞结构与稳定性的影响。

Method: 通过通用熵延迟函数$F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$构造对称cut-and-paste虫洞，将Darmois-Israel junction条件用延迟函数和熵导数表达，推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒方程、径向有效势，并应用于8种熵模型。

Result: 对称构造在所有允许的正延迟域中都需要负表面能密度；熵变形显著改变视界结构、允许喉部区域和表面应力分布；所有常数barotropic分支线性径向不稳定；变Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性由熵几何、喉部半径和态方程径向指数共同决定。

Conclusion: 建立了比较熵诱导黑洞几何作为薄壳虫洞种子的统一框架，表明稳定性不仅由熵变形单独决定，还取决于熵变形与喉部局域物质动力学响应的相互作用。

Abstract: Modified black-hole entropies can induce effective spacetime geometries and thereby provide a thermodynamic route for investigating thin-shell wormholes. In this work, we construct symmetric cut-and-paste wormholes from the generic entropic lapse function $F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$ and formulate the Darmois--Israel junction conditions directly in terms of the lapse and of the entropy derivatives. We derive the surface stresses, shell energy-condition combinations, conservation equation, and radial effective potential, and then apply the formalism to the Bekenstein--Hawking, Barrow, Tsallis--Cirto, Rényi, Kaniadakis, logarithmic, loop-quantum-gravity-inspired, and exponential entropy prescriptions. The analysis shows that the symmetric construction requires negative surface energy density throughout every admissible positive-lapse domain, although entropy deformations can significantly modify the horizon structure, the allowed throat region, and the localization of the surface stresses. Within the parameter domains considered here, all examined constant-barotropic branches are linearly radially unstable, despite quantitative changes in their near-horizon scales. In contrast, a variable Chaplygin shell can support stable configurations, with the stability domains determined jointly by the entropic geometry, the throat radius, and the radial exponent of the shell equation of state. These results establish a unified framework for comparing entropy-induced black-hole geometries as thin-shell wormhole seeds and show that stability is governed not by the entropy deformation alone, but by its interplay with the dynamical response of the matter localized at the throat.

</details>


### [5] [A note on the conformal geometry of the 2-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14133)
*Jörg Frauendiener*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究二维球面上将给定度规通过共形因子缩放为圆度规的几何问题，发现了一个独特的对称无迹张量，并推导出具有闵可夫斯基结构的线性二阶偏微分方程，最终证明了每个二维球面都能以典范方式等距嵌入闵可夫斯基空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维球面上共形因子将给定度规缩放为圆度规的几何结构，寻找其内在的对称性和不变性。

Method: 通过发现一个补充Ricci张量构成无散度对称张量的独特对称无迹张量，推导线性二阶偏微分方程，分析其解空间的闵可夫斯基结构，并研究等距嵌入。

Result: 存在唯一对称无迹张量；导出的线性二阶PDE解空间具有闵可夫斯基结构；存在无穷多个这样的空间，由"4-矢量"参数化的等距关联；每个2-球面可典范等距嵌入闵可夫斯基空间。

Conclusion: 二维球面的共形几何与闵可夫斯基空间之间存在深刻的联系，揭示了球面几何中隐藏的洛伦兹结构。

Abstract: We discuss the geometry of the two-dimensional sphere. Our perspective focuses on the set of conformal factors that rescale a given metric to a round metric. We find that there exists a unique symmetric and trace-free tensor on the sphere which complements the Ricci tensor to a divergence-free symmetric tensor. Using this tensor, we derive a linear second-order PDE whose solution space carries a natural Minkowski structure. We show that there are in fact infinitely many such spaces, all related by isometries which can be thought of as being parametrised by ``4-vectors''. Finally, we show that every 2-sphere can be isometrically embedded in a Minkowski space in a canonical way.

</details>

*Syed Moeez Hassan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文献通过数值方法发现反弹时物质能量密度与各向异性剪切之间存在某种关系，但缺乏严格的理论证明。本文旨在从理论上推广有效弗里德曼和Raychaudhuri方程，并严格证明该关系的普适性。

Method: 采用聚合物量子化方法处理Bianchi-I时空，推导广义有效弗里德曼方程和Raychaudhuri方程；通过分析方程结构，解析地证明反弹点处能量密度与剪切的关系，并导出其系数的显式形式。

Result: 成功推导了广义有效场方程；证明反弹时能量密度与各向异性剪切的关系对于任意物质内容均精确成立；给出了该关系中常数的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为聚合物量子宇宙学中各向异性反弹的普适性提供了严格的理论基础，阐明了反弹物理中关键参数的解析来源。

Abstract: We derive generalized effective Friedmann and Raychaudhuri equations for a bouncing polymer quantized Bianchi-I spacetime. We further prove that the relation numerically derived in the literature between the matter energy density and the anisotropic shear at the bounce holds exactly for any type of matter content, and derive explicit expressions for the constants that appear in that relation.

</details>


### [7] [Reconstructions of Horndeski Subclasses by Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14154)
*Marco C. Mendoza Marcos,Ana A. Avilez Lopez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，基于宇宙学观测数据（宇宙计时器、重子声学振荡），重建了Horndeski理论的两个子类（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon），并发现Brans-Dicke子类在红移0.4-1.7范围内优于ΛCDM模型，Cubic Galileon子类则在整个观测红移范围内倾向于标量场与引力耦合且暗能量密度随时间演化的修正 cosmology。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索最一般的标量-张量引力理论（Horndeski类）中两个子类（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon）是否能够更好地解释晚期宇宙的观测数据，特别是检验其相对于标准ΛCDM模型的优劣，并考察引力常数随时间变化以及暗能量密度演化的可能性。

Method: 采用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，利用宇宙计时器（Cosmic Chronometers）提供的模型无关哈勃率测量数据，以及重子声学振荡（BAO）提供的哈勃率和距离测量数据，对Horndeski理论中的Brans-Dicke子类和Cubic Galileon子类进行重建。

Result: 对于Brans-Dicke子类，在红移范围z∈(0.4, 1.7)内，最可能的高斯过程重建倾向于该子类优于ΛCDM模型；对于Cubic Galileon子类，最可能的重建表明在整个观测红移范围内，标量场与引力的修正以及随时间演化的暗能量密度是更受偏好的。

Conclusion: 两类修正引力理论均在特定红移范围内比ΛCDM模型更好地拟合了观测数据，表明晚期宇宙可能存在超出ΛCDM的引力修正和暗能量演化行为，且这些理论同时满足引力波传播速度的约束。

Abstract: In this work we reconstruct two subclasses of theories from the Horndeski class, the most general scalar-tensor theories of gravity with second-order equations, according to a variety of late-universe cosmological data: model independent measurements of Hubble rate from Cosmic Chronometers, as well as Hubble rate and distance measurements from Baryonic Acoustic Oscillations. In the modified gravity family considered, the scalar field is non-minimally coupled to gravity leading to time variations in the gravitational constant at cosmological scales. The theories considered are also consistent with constraints on the propagation speed of gravitational waves. The reconstruction is carried out by using the Gaussian Processes bayesian technique either for the Brans-Dicke and the Cubic Galileon subclasses of Horndeski theory. We find that the most likely Gaussian Process reconstruction for the data favors a cosmology from the Brans-Dicke subclass over $Λ$CDM in the range $z \in (0.4, 1.7)$, and for the Cubic Galileon subclass we find that the most likely reconstruction indeed favors modifications of the scalar field to gravity with time evolving dark energy density in the whole range of redshift observations.

</details>


### [8] [Breaking the Degeneracy: Spectral Hardening of Accretion Disks around Rotating Hayward Black Holes in Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14164)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入宏观暗物质晕的自转正则Hayward黑洞周围薄吸积盘的热力学与观测特征。通过构建三区域复合时空模型，计算了ISCO、辐射效率、热通量及多色黑体光谱的修正。结果表明量子修正核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率有叠加增强作用，但导致了几何与天体物理配置间的宏观简并性，这种简并仅在极端Wien尾处被打破，为区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境提供了关键诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究正则黑洞（Hayward黑洞）与暗物质晕共存时的吸积盘可观测特征，并解决量子引力修正与暗物质环境在观测信号上的简并性问题。

Method: 构建三区域复合时空模型（内区Hayward核心+中间暗物质壳层+外渐近平坦真空），通过质量分布函数匹配各区域边界，计算ISCO、辐射效率、局部热通量及多色黑体光谱，并对比四种构型（Kerr真空、Hayward真空、Kerr+暗物质、Hayward+暗物质）。

Result: 自旋参数j=0.8时辐射效率可达约14.4%；量子核心与暗物质晕对ISCO有严格的层次压缩效应；低至中频段存在纯几何（Hayward真空）与纯天体物理（Kerr+暗物质）构型间的简并性，该简并在极端Wien尾处被打破。

Conclusion: 高能谱偏振测量（特别是极端Wien尾波段）是区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境的关键观测手段；量子核心与经典暗物质晕的共存产生可检测的叠加信号，但需注意参数简并导致的误判风险。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and observable signatures of thin accretion disks surrounding rotating, regular Hayward black holes embedded within a macroscopic dark matter (DM) envelope. The spacetime is rigorously modelled as a three-region composite: an inner Hayward core regularised by a de~Sitter limit, an intermediate DM shell modelled as a pressureless dust envelope governed by an exponential sphere density profile, and an asymptotically flat outer vacuum. By matching these regions via the mass profile, we compute the explicit modifications to the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO), radiative efficiency, local thermal flux, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity. A systematic comparison across four distinct configurations (Kerr vacuum, Hayward vacuum, Kerr\,+\,DM, and Hayward\,+\,DM) reveals a strict hierarchical compression of the ISCO. We demonstrate that the quantum-inspired core regularity and the macroscopic DM halo exert an additive enhancement on the radiative efficiency, reaching $\sim 14.4\%$ for highly spinning black holes ($j=0.8$). However, this additive behaviour introduces a profound macroscopic degeneracy between purely geometric (Hayward vacuum) and purely astrophysical (Kerr\,+\,DM) configurations at low-to-intermediate frequencies. We establish that this degeneracy is structurally broken only in the extreme Wien tail of the multi-wavelength spectrum, providing a critical diagnostic footprint for future high-frequency spectropolarimetry to distinguish non-singular black holes from classical dark matter environments.

</details>


### [9] [Probing Quantum Gravity through Chaotic Orbits and Strong-Field Effects in Kerr Black Holes Embedded in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14170)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用庞加莱截面、李雅普诺夫指数、KS熵和加权伯克霍夫平均等多种方法，研究了量子改进旋转黑洞被完美流体暗物质（PFDM）包围时的非线性光子动力学。结果表明量子改进参数和PFDM参数会增强光子轨迹对初始条件的敏感性，导致相空间从规则运动向混沌运动转变，并对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜产生可能的观测影响。

  <summary>Details</summary>

The resulting LQG-corrected Kerr geometry is used to analyze orbital motion, revealing the interplay between spin and quantum corrections in strong-field trajectories. Our results indicate that EMRI observations provide a promising avenue to probe quantum gravitational effects in black-hole spacetimes.

Abstract: Condensed mathematics is a framework developed by Clausen and Scholze for handling algebraic structure in the presence of topological information, using methods from homological algebra and sheaf theory. It emphasizes accessibility and includes many details intended to assist readers approaching the theory for the first time. The second part aims to show a particular application of the framework. Abstract: We generalize work of Wei-Wu on Viehweg hyperbolicity for families of stable pairs over smooth projective varieties to the case of smooth Deligne-Mumford stacks. Abstract: The first main theme of this paper is to study symplectic (un-)knottedness of Lagrangian submanifolds which are not tori. Our construction of such Lagrangians is inspired by the generalized Polterovich Lagrangians of Oakley--Usher and relies on an adaption of symplectic reduction methods going back to Chekanov--Schlenk and McDuff's probes to the case of non-abelian group actions. We compare Oakley--Usher Lagrangians to their (un-)knotted cousins to find that, depending on the situation, they are: (1) not diffeomorphic, (2) diffeomorphic, but not Lagrangian isotopic; (3) Lagrangian isotopic, but not Hamiltonian isotopic, (4) Hamiltonian isotopic. This relationship between them frequently changes when the ambient space is compactified.
  The second main theme is an in-depth study of monotone Lagrangian tori obtained from appyling our constructions to the three-dimensional quadric $Q^3$. Computing the versal deformation of the $Ψ$-invariant of Shelukhin--Tonkonog--Vianna, we find a torus which is knotted in a strong sense: it is not Hamiltonian isotopic to the Biran-lift of any Vianna torus in the two-dimensional quadric. Furthermore, we investigate enumerative properties of the Oakley--Usher torus in $Q^3$ and prove its non-displaceability, settling an open question asked by Oakley--Usher. We relate this to mirror symmetry by proving a split-generation result for the monotone Fukaya category of $Q^3$, which is inspired by, and can be compared to a similar result of Abouzaid--Diogo for cotangent bundles of the sphere.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动态量子引力中观测者的体验，特别是将量子力学系统耦合到JT引力的效应。

Result: 引力导致观测者的欧几里得时间（逆温度）涨落，演化算符变为加权平均；涨落相比均值很小但相比普朗克尺度较大；双喇叭图中温度涨落很大，不存在光滑半经典鞍点。

Abstract: Motivated by the problem of understanding the experience of an observer in dynamical quantum gravity, we study the effects of coupling a one-dimensional quantum mechanical system living on a bulk worldline to Euclidean AdS JT gravity. Abstract: Open systems are ubiquitous in physics. Many realistic systems interact, at least weakly, with environmental degrees of freedom that may be too numerous, too complicated, inaccessible, or unknown. When only a subset of degrees of freedom is observed, its reduced dynamics can differ qualitatively from that of a closed system, displaying dissipation, noise, decoherence, memory effects, or loss of information into unobserved sectors. When the microscopic description is also unknown, one is led to an open effective description, in which the relevant degrees of freedom are treated systematically while the environment and the microscopics are parametrized rather than solved for explicitly.

The main open problems of cosmology, including inflation, dark matter, and dark energy, involve spacetime-filling sectors whose microscopic nature is unknown and whose observed effects are primarily gravitational. At the same time, gravitational systems often lack a preferred notion of conserved energy because they are time dependent, and naturally display out-of-equilibrium dynamics. These lecture notes introduce the operator formalism and the Schwinger-Keldysh path-integral as tools to study open systems, with emphasis on open effective field theories and inflation. They are aimed at master students, PhD students, and researchers approaching these topics for the first time.

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect for a massive real scalar field confined between two perfectly reflecting parallel plates in the presence of a position-dependent effective mass, a mechanism for coupling a scalar background to a scalar field. Upon quantization of the field, the vacuum energy is evaluated by means of generalized zeta-function regularization together with an appropriate renormalization procedure. The renormalized vacuum energy naturally separates into a Landau-like contribution and an additional term induced by the spatial dependence of the effective mass. We show analytically and numerically that both contributions are exponentially suppressed in the strong-coupling regime. In the opposite limit, the Landau-like contribution smoothly reproduces the standard vacuum energy for a massive scalar field confined between parallel plates, whereas the additional contribution becomes singular owing to the restricted domain of validity of the exact spectrum. Except in the vicinity of this singular limit, the vacuum energy is shown to be dominated by the Landau-like sector. Our results establish a direct connection between position-dependent effective masses and boundary-induced quantum vacuum phenomena, providing a new exactly solvable framework for the investigation of Casimir effects in spatially inhomogeneous relativistic systems.

From time to time it has been suggested that valuable insight into these conceptual challenges could come from the analysis of appropriately designed first-quantized toy models, in which major technical and interpretive issues can be effectively managed or sidestepped altogether. The specific spacetime noncommutativity implemented in our model, which we call 'time-commutative $κ$-plane', had already been proposed as a suitable arena for a first-quantized analysis, but previous studies were mostly heuristic and failed to provide a full description of the deformed relativistic symmetries. The scope of optical dump searches is extended to both the ALP-photon coupling induced Primakoff process and the Compton-like scattering via the ALP-electron coupling. Abstract: We investigate spin-resolved decay of an axion-like particle (ALP) into an electron--positron pair in an intense laser field. Using the Baier--Katkov quasiclassical operator formalism and the locally constant field approximation, we derive a compact analytic rate retaining finite ALP-mass effects and the lepton spin degrees of freedom. In the massless limit, the spin-summed rate has the same weak- and strong-field asymptotic scalings as the corresponding photon-induced pair-creation rate, while the pseudoscalar coupling induces distinct spin-resolved channels and spin correlations. A finite ALP mass reorganizes the spectrum across the vacuum threshold, producing purely field-induced pair creation below threshold and spin-dependent oscillatory modulations above threshold through the coherent interplay of vacuum and field-assisted contributions. The entanglement of the produced pair reflects the dominant production mechanism. Near the vacuum threshold in weak fields, the pair is nearly maximally entangled and singlet-like. Away from threshold, the reduced spin state becomes triplet-like, retaining a concurrence of \(1/2\) when strong-field production dominates but becoming separable when vacuum decay dominates. These results identify spin-resolved spectra and entanglement as signatures of finite-mass and threshold effects in strong-field ALP searches.

</details>

Conclusion: 热质量降低了摩擦计算对等离子体中难以控制的红外扇区的敏感性，提高了壁速计算的可靠性。

Abstract: Bubble-wall friction controls the dynamics of first-order cosmological phase transitions. In Boltzmann-equation approaches, a major uncertainty arises from infrared gauge bosons, whose contribution is artificially enhanced in the massless approximation. We study the impact of thermal masses by including them consistently in both the Liouville operator and the collision integrals. Thermal masses suppress the source term for out-of-equilibrium perturbations while also reducing interaction rates. These effects largely cancel for top quarks, giving only percent-level changes, but they strongly suppress the infrared gauge-boson contribution, shifting the dominant momenta to scales of order the temperature. As a result, gauge bosons become subleading and wall velocities are close to those obtained from top-quark friction alone. We illustrate this in the singlet-extended Standard Model. Our results show that thermal masses reduce the sensitivity of friction calculations to the poorly controlled infrared sector of the plasma.

Abstract: In this work, we perform a systematic investigation of the hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark states, encompassing both bound states and resonances, which originate from the $P$-wave interactions between ground-state bottom baryons ($Λ_b$, $Σ_b^{(*)}$) and ground-state antibottom mesons ($B^{(*)}$). Adopting the one-boson-exchange model and including the coupled-channel effects, we derive the effective potentials for all allowed quantum numbers $I(J^P) = 1/2(1/2^+)$, $1/2(3/2^+)$, $1/2(5/2^+)$, $1/2(7/2^+)$, $3/2(1/2^+)$, $3/2(3/2^+)$, $3/2(5/2^+)$, and $3/2(7/2^+)$. We then solve the coupled-channel Schrödinger equations to search for the bound-state solutions and perform the phase-shift analyses to identify resonance poles. Our results reveal a rich spectrum of positive-parity hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark candidates. In the isospin $I=1/2$ sector, we find several loosely bound states and associated resonances, particularly in the $Σ_b B^*$ and $Σ_b^* B^*$ channels, where the coupled-channel dynamics plays an essential role in their formation. In the isospin $I=3/2$ sector, the attraction is generally weaker because of the isospin factors. Nevertheless, we still obtain the loosely bound states and resonances, such as the $Σ_b^* B^*$ states with $J^P=3/2^+$, $5/2^+$, and $7/2^+$. The prominence of high-spin partial waves, for instance the $^6P_J$ components, underscores the importance of the spin-spin and tensor interactions. Our predictions provide a comprehensive and systematic spectrum of the $P$-wave hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark states and offer clear guidance for future experimental searches at LHCb and Belle~II.

Abstract: We show the results from a comprehensive study of the gluonic structure of the proton at small $x$ extracted from exclusive $J/ψ$ production at the H1 and ZEUS experiments at HERA. The model shows an excellent fit to data with $χ^2/{\rm ndf}=0.77$. We find that the hotspots have a perturbative Gaussian core of size $0.105(1)$ fm, surrounded by a non-perturbative exponential halo of 0.220(2) fm. Abstract: In this work, we investigate a cross-section-ratio-based inversion method for extracting distribution functions at the Electron--Ion Collider (EIC). Starting from the general factorization formula $dσ= H \otimes f$, we show that, for an appropriate bremsstrahlung process and suitably chosen differential observables, the convolution structure can be reduced to a multiplicative form. On this basis, we define the ratio $R = \frac{dσ_{\mathrm{exp}}}{dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}},$ where $dσ_{\mathrm{exp}}$ is the experimentally measured differential cross section and $dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}$ is a perturbatively calculable theoretical input. We then establish the relation between $R$ and the target distribution function $f$. Finally, we investigate how the theoretical input $dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}$ should be constructed when soft-photon radiation, finite-bin-width effects, and experimental acceptance are taken into account.

Abstract: We study exclusive electroproduction of heavy quark-antiquark pairs off nucleons in the framework of generalized transverse momentum dependent parton distributions (GTMDs) for gluons. The short-distance part of the process is treated at leading order in perturbative Quantum Chromodynamics and in first order in a collinear expansion, which allows identification with the description in terms of Generalized Parton Distributions (GPDs). For the results for the structure functions in terms of GTMDs and GPDs we consider only unpolarized (spin-averaged) nucleons, but include all possible azimuthal modulations that can arise. The presented results extend known expressions in the literature and are relevant for experimental studies of this exclusive process at the future Electron Ion Collider. Furthermore, we introduce a convenient decomposition of the gluon-gluon correlation matrix in terms of GTMDs, expanded in a Lorentz basis of symmetric traceless tensors obtained from the partonic momentum $k_T$ and the momentum transfer $Δ_T$. The adopted notation for the GTMDs relates to the nucleon helicity states at the amplitude level, rather than to polarization states of the incoming nucleon or of the gluons, which makes it more transparent which contributions from helicity difference and helicity flip matrix elements can be accessed with unpolarized nucleon beams.

This explains why the Wilson-line calculation captures the complete kinematic dependence, thereby accounting for the observed universality across gauge theories.

Abstract: We study quantum spin correlations in top-quark pair production at future lepton colliders in the presence of a neutral gauge boson from anomaly-free general $U(1)$ extensions of the Standard Model. Abstract: Collinear splitting functions govern parton evolution, parton showers, and resummation at high-energy colliders. We consider the radiative decay of the $Z$ boson as a representative example and perform a detailed analytical study of the $τ^-τ^+γ$ spin state, as determined by the chiral interactions of the Standard Model, over the complete three-body phase space. We explore various quantum information observables to quantify how the $τ^-τ^+$ spin state changes with the photon radiation. We find some striking features, for example that the emitted photon could either lead to decoherence or monotonic enhancement of entanglement for the fermion pair.

</details>

*Vidas Regelskis*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究BI、CI、DI型分裂对称对对应的扭曲Yangian，引入了一种新的转置表示（transposed presentation），该表示由扭曲反射方程控制，并与生成矩阵的高斯分解自然交互。完全在R-矩阵表示框架内，推导了特殊扭曲Yangian SY^tw(g_N)和扩展扭曲Yangian X^tw(g_N)的Drinfeld型电流表示，其中Serre关系以封闭电流形式给出。提取系数恢复了Lu提出的Drinfeld表示。作为推论，建立了由Lu、Wang和Zhang猜想的这些扭曲Yangian的R-矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示之间的同构。作为副产品，得到了X^tw(g_N)的张量积分解（分解为Drinfeld表示中的扭曲Yangian与可数多个中心变量的多项式环），以及Drinfeld生成元的PBW基，并描述了低Drinfeld模上的余理想余乘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为BI、CI、DI型分裂对称对对应的扭曲Yangian建立R-矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示之间的严格同构，解决Lu、Wang和Zhang提出的猜想，并为这些代数提供更完备的结构理论。

Method: 引入由扭曲反射方程控制的转置表示，利用高斯分解技术，在R-矩阵表示框架内推导Drinfeld型电流表示，通过系数提取恢复已知的Drinfeld表示，并建立PBW基和余乘结构。

Result: 提出了扭曲Yangian的新表示（转置表示）；推导了SY^tw(g_N)和X^tw(g_N)的Drinfeld型电流表示，Serre关系以封闭电流形式给出；建立了R-矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示的同构；得到了X^tw(g_N)的张量积分解；构造了Drinfeld生成元的PBW基；描述了低Drinfeld模上的余理想余乘。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对BI、CI、DI型扭曲Yangian的R-矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示之间同构的严格证明，证实了Lu、Wang和Zhang的猜想，并为后续研究这些代数的表示论和量子对称性提供了重要的结构工具。

Abstract: We study twisted Yangians associated with the split symmetric pairs of types BI, CI and DI. We introduce a new presentation of these algebras, which we call the transposed presentation, governed by a twisted reflection equation that interacts naturally with the Gaussian decomposition of the generating matrix. Working entirely within the $R$-matrix presentation, we derive Drinfeld-type current presentations of the special twisted Yangian $SY^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$ and of the extended twisted Yangian $X^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$, in which the Serre relations are stated in a closed current form. Extracting coefficients recovers the Drinfeld presentation due to Lu. As a consequence, we establish the isomorphism between the $R$-matrix and Drinfeld presentations of these twisted Yangians conjectured by Lu, Wang and Zhang. As a byproduct, we obtain a tensor product decomposition of $X^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$ into the twisted Yangian in the Drinfeld presentation and a polynomial ring in countably many central variables. We also obtain Poincaré-Birkhoff-Witt bases in the Drinfeld generators and describe the coideal coproduct on the low Drinfeld modes.

</details>

### [85] [Cluster structures on $SL_n/SO_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14634)
*Jeff York Ye*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文通过在 $SL_n$ 上通过折叠方法构造了对称空间 $SL_n/SO_n$ 的分层流形 $\mathring{S}_w$ 上的簇结构，并证明了这些簇结构与 De Concini Poisson 结构兼容，同时将簇结构推广到了对称矩阵簇 $\text{Sym}_n$ 上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对称空间 $SL_n/SO_n$ 上的簇结构，并将其与 Poisson 结构联系起来，从而推广到更广泛的矩阵簇上。

Method: 通过研究上层簇代数的折叠方法，将 $SL_n$ 上的簇结构通过 folding 操作转化为 $SL_n/SO_n$ 上的簇结构。

Result: 成功地在 $SL_n/SO_n$ 的分层流形 $\mathring{S}_w$ 上构造了簇结构，并证明了这些结构与 De Concini Poisson 结构兼容，同时构造了对称矩阵簇 $\text{Sym}_n$ 上的簇结构。

Conclusion: 簇结构可以从 $SL_n$ 通过折叠传递到 $SL_n/SO_n$，且与 Poisson 几何兼容，为对称空间的簇结构研究提供了新方法。

Abstract: We construct cluster structures on the strata $\mathring{S}_w$ of a stratification of the symmetric space $SL_n/SO_n$ over $\mathbb{C}$. To accomplish this, we study foldings of upper cluster algebras and show that the cluster structures on $SL_n/SO_n$ can be obtained from those on $SL_n$ via folding. As a corollary, we show that these cluster structures are compatible with the De Concini Poisson structures, and we construct cluster structures on the variety of symmetric matrices $\text{Sym}_n$.

</details>


### [86] [A superplethystic formula for superrestriction coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14939)
*Tirtharaj Basu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schur超代数$S(m,n;d)$中不可约超模限制到子群$S_{m}\times S_{n}$的系数问题，引入了超重言式（superplethysm）概念，给出了Littlewood公式的超重言变体，并应用于证明二分超分拆的单峰性结果。


<details>

Motivation: 确定Schur超代数$S(m,n;d)$的不可约超模在限制到其乘法子群$S_{m}\times S_{n}$时的分解系数，这是表示论中经典的限制问题在超代数情境下的推广。

Method: 引入超重言式（superplethysm）的概念，并推导出Littlewood公式的超重言变体，用以计算限制系数。

Result: 得到了限制系数的显式表达（超重言式版本的Littlewood公式），并以此证明了二分超分拆的若干单峰性结果。

Conclusion: 超重言式为计算超代数限制系数提供了有效工具，并揭示了二分超分拆的组合性质。

Abstract: The Schur superalgebra $S(m,n;d)$ contains $S_{m}\times S_{n}$ as a multiplicative subgroup. We consider the problem of determining the restriction coefficients when an irreducible supermodule of $S(m,n;d)$ is restricted to $S_{m}\times S_{n}$. We introduce the notion of superplethysm and provide a superplethystic variant of Littlewood's formula for these restriction coefficients. As an application, we provide several unimodality results for bipartite superpartitions.

</details>

Method: 定义了调和变量的概念，并针对具有双不变度量的李群及特定齐性空间（如对称空间）提出了构造此类变量的算法。

Result: 调和变量能够用于构造一类广泛非线性微分方程的特解，并在李群和齐性空间上给出了可操作的构造方法。

Conclusion: 调和变量为求解非线性微分方程提供了一种新的系统性方法，特别适用于具有对称性的几何空间。

Abstract: A definition of harmonic variables for Laplace operators on Riemannian and pseudo-Riemannian spaces is introduced. Harmonic variables allow, in particular, the construction of particular (non-invariant group) solutions to a wide class of nonlinear differential equations. For Lie groups with a bi-invariant metric and a certain class of homogeneous spaces (e.g., symmetric spaces), an algorithm for constructing such variables is presented.

</details>


### [88] [A Self-Dual Frame Formalism of the SO(3) Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14204)
*Hanwen Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究闭定向黎曼4-流形上SO(3)联络的Yang-Mills方程，通过将联络简化为正定对称矩阵场h，证明了Yang-Mills方程等价于一个确定的二阶系统Φ_g(h)=0，并建立了变分公式、自动不可约性、椭圆正则性和线性化算子的Fredholm指标定理。特别地，对于h=e^{2ω}g的形式，方程等价于反自对偶和常数量曲率条件，从而反自对偶共形类给出全局解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立闭定向黎曼4-流形上SO(3)联络的Yang-Mills方程与一个由正定对称矩阵场表示的确定二阶系统之间的等价关系，从而提供一种新的分析框架。

Method: 利用自对偶2-形式丛Λ^+上的SO(3)联络，在自对偶曲率构成定向标架的开放区域上，通过点态极分解消除规范自由度，将联络简化为正定对称矩阵场h；证明了h唯一确定相容联络A(h)，并将Yang-Mills方程转化为Φ_g(h)=0的二阶系统。

Result: 建立了Yang-Mills方程等价于Φ_g(h)=0这一完全确定的二阶系统；获得了变分公式、自动不可约性、椭圆正则性和线性化算子的Fredholm指标定理；证明了当h=e^{2ω}g时，方程等价于反自对偶性加上常数量曲率6√2的条件。

Conclusion: 每个具有正Yamabe不变量的反自对偶共形类都给出该方程的一个全局解，揭示了Yang-Mills理论与共形几何之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: For a closed oriented Riemannian $4$-manifold $(M,g)$, we consider $\operatorname{SO}(3)$ connections on the bundle $Λ^+$ of self-dual $2$-forms. On the open locus where the self-dual curvature is an orientation-preserving frame, pointwise polar decomposition removes the gauge freedom and replaces the connection by a field $h$ of positive definite symmetric matrices. We show that $h$ determines a unique compatible connection $A(h)$ and that the Yang--Mills equation is equivalent to the exactly determined second order system $$ Φ_g(h):=F_{A(h)}^+h^{-1}-g=0. $$ We establish a variational formulation, automatic irreducibility, elliptic regularity, and a Fredholm index theorem for the linearized operator. For matrix fields of the form $h=e^{2ω}g$, the equation $Φ_g(h)=0$ is equivalent to anti-self-duality and constant scalar curvature $6\sqrt{2}$. Consequently, every anti-self-dual conformal class of positive Yamabe invariant gives a global solution.

</details>


### [89] [Scalar Curvature and Product Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14244)
*Claude LeBrun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了4维光滑紧致流形M，其欧拉示性数为零且不承认真数量曲率黎曼度量，但M×S¹却承认真数量曲率度量，从而给出了Jie Xu提出的受限Rosenberg S¹-稳定性猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Jie Xu近期提出的Rosenberg S¹-稳定性猜想的受限形式是否成立，即验证欧拉示性数为零的4维流形是否满足该猜想。

Method: 通过构造具体的4维光滑紧致流形M（欧拉示性数为零），分别检验M本身及其与S¹的笛卡尔积M×S¹是否承认真数量曲率度量，以此构造反例。

Result: 找到了4维反例：M不承认真数量曲率度量，但M×S¹承认真数量曲率度量。

Conclusion: Jie Xu提出的受限Rosenberg S¹-稳定性猜想在4维情形下不成立，即欧拉示性数为零这一条件不足以保证该猜想的成立。

Abstract: We display 4-dimensional counter-examples to a restricted form of Rosenberg's S^1-Stability Conjecture recently proposed by Jie Xu. Namely, we show that there are smooth compact 4-manifolds M with Euler characteristic zero that do not admit positive-scalar-curvature Riemannian metrics, but for which the corresponding Cartesian products M x S^1 nevertheless do admit such metrics.

</details>


### [90] [Lipschitz continuity of the cut time for globally hyperbolic spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14374)
*Alec Metsch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在全局双曲时空中，焦化时间在有限区域内是局部Lipschitz连续的，切割时间在包含其对应测地线的邻域内也是局部Lipschitz连续的，并给出了零锥附近的Lipschitz常数定量估计，最后证明了焦散集的Hausdorff余维数至少为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黎曼流形上Itoh-Tanaka和Li-Nirenberg关于Lipschitz连续性的经典结果推广到洛伦兹流形（全局双曲时空）中，研究焦化时间和切割时间的正则性。

Method: 采用Itoh-Tanaka的方法，并适当调整适应洛伦兹背景。通过分析未来因果锥上的开子集、零锥附近的定量估计等技巧来证明局部Lipschitz连续性。

Result: (1) 全局双曲时空中固定点的焦化时间在有限区域内局部Lipschitz连续；(2) 切割时间在包含其对应测地线的邻域内局部Lipschitz连续；(3) 零锥附近给出了Lipschitz常数的定量估计；(4) 提供了Lipschitz性质延伸到零方向的判据；(5) 焦散集的Hausdorff余维数至少为1。

Conclusion: 成功将黎曼几何中的Lipschitz连续性结果推广到洛伦兹几何中，为全局双曲时空中焦散集和切割点的几何分析提供了正则性基础。

Abstract: We prove that, for a fixed point in a globally hyperbolic spacetime, the focalization time is locally Lipschitz continuous on the open subset of the future causal cone where it is finite. We also show that the cut time is locally Lipschitz continuous in a neighborhood of any timelike tangent vector whose associated geodesic is defined at least up to (and including) its cut time. Furthermore, we derive quantitative estimates for the Lipschitz constant near the null cone and provide a criterion ensuring that the Lipschitz property extends to null directions. As a consequence, we show that the cut locus of a point has Hausdorff codimension at least $1$. These results extend classical Lipschitz continuity results for complete Riemannian manifolds due to Itoh-Tanaka and Li-Nirenberg. Our approach follows the method of Itoh-Tanaka, suitably adapted to the Lorentzian setting.

</details>


### [91] [The existence of $k$-convex hypersurface for a class of Hessian curvature equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14717)
*Kang Xiao,Jiabao Gong*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Hessian曲率方程闭星形超曲面的存在性，通过先验估计与连续性方法相结合，建立了非齐次和齐次Hessian曲率方程k-凸超曲面的存在唯一性，并通过常数秩定理证明了所得k-凸超曲面是严格凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hessian曲率方程闭星形超曲面的存在性问题，这是几何分析中的重要课题，涉及非线性偏微分方程和微分几何的交叉领域。

Method: 采用先验估计与连续性方法相结合的策略，同时建立常数秩定理来证明超曲面的严格凸性。

Result: 建立了非齐次和齐次Hessian曲率方程k-凸超曲面的存在唯一性，并证明了这些超曲面是严格凸的。

Conclusion: 成功证明了在适当条件下，Hessian曲率方程存在唯一的k-凸闭星形超曲面，且该曲面具有严格凸性。

Abstract: This article investigates the existence of closed, star-shaped hypersurfaces for a class of Hessian curvature equations. By combining a priori estimates with the continuity method, we establish the existence and uniqueness of $k$-convex hypersurfaces for both nonhomogeneous and homogeneous Hessian curvature equations, and by establishing a constant rank theorem, we prove that the resulting $k$-convex hypersurfaces are strictly convex.

</details>


### [92] [A framework for producing harmonic maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14857)
*Volker Branding,Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于从固定调和映射生成从单位球到欧几里得球面的一系列调和映射族，为Nakauchi最近引入的广义径向投影提供了理论支撑，并展示了这些映射族如何用于构造内在双调和映射、三调和映射、p-调和映射以及外在多调和映射等变分问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为生成从单位球到欧几里得球面的调和映射族提供一个通用理论框架，并为Nakauchi的广义径向投影建立理论基础。

Method: 基于Toth建立的从给定特征映射生成球面间特征映射的机制，扩展为生成调和映射族的通用框架。

Result: 成功建立了生成调和映射族的通用框架，并揭示了这些映射族可用于构造多种变分问题的解，包括内在双调和映射、三调和映射、p-调和映射以及外在多调和映射。

Conclusion: 该框架为调和映射族的构造提供了理论基础，并展示了其在解决相关变分问题中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a general framework for producing families of harmonic maps from the unit ball into the Euclidean sphere starting from a fixed harmonic map. In particular, our analysis provides a theoretical background for a generalized radial projection which was recently introduced by Nakauchi.
  Our approach is based on work of Toth who established a machinery that generates eigenmaps between spheres from a given eigenmap.
  Finally, we point out how these families of harmonic maps can be used to construct solutions to associated variational problems such as intrinsic biharmonic and triharmonic maps, $p$-harmonic maps as well as extrinsic polyharmonic maps.

</details>


### [93] [A quantitative Schur comparison theorem for curves in CAT(k) spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15106)
*Mohammad Ghomi,John Ioannis Stavroulakis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了CAT(k)空间中具有有限总曲率曲线的Schur比较定理的定量形式，推广了欧氏空间中的经典臂引理和弓引理及其黎曼类比。证明基于模型平面中曲线的比较公式，该公式用曲率测度和力学中的矩臂概念表示，并结合了Reshetnyak定理的改进形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schur比较定理在欧氏空间和黎曼流形中已有重要应用，但缺乏在更一般的CAT(k)空间中的定量刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出更精确的比较结果。

Method: 1. 建立模型平面中曲线的比较公式，引入曲率测度和矩臂概念；2. 改进Reshetnyak定理以控制优曲线的曲率；3. 结合上述工具得到定量Schur比较定理。

Result: 得到了CAT(k)空间中有限总曲率曲线的Schur比较定理的定量形式，锐化和推广了经典臂引理、弓引理及其黎曼类比。

Conclusion: 该定量形式丰富了CAT(k)空间中的曲线比较理论，为相关几何分析提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: We obtain a quantitative form of Schur's comparison theorem for curves with finite total curvature in CAT(k) spaces. This sharpens and extends the classical arm and bow lemmas in Euclidean space, as well as their Riemannian analogues. The proof is based on a comparison formula for curves in model planes, expressed in terms of curvature measures and a notion of moment arm borrowed from mechanics. Another ingredient is a refinement of Reshetnyak's theorem that controls the curvature of the majorizing curve.

</details>


### [94] [On the Darboux Integrability of the Constant Mean Curvature One Equation for Surfaces in Hyperbolic 3 space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15244)
*Mark E. Fels,Thomas A. Ivey*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文通过群论构造证明了决定双曲3空间中常平均曲率浸入的椭圆偏微分方程组等价于椭圆Liouville方程，并给出了具体的等价映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立双曲3空间中常平均曲率浸入问题与椭圆Liouville方程之间的等价关系，以利用后者的已知性质来研究前者。

Method: 使用群论构造，通过Darboux可积性分析，证明每个方程存在等价的商表示，并构建显式的等价映射。

Result: 证明了常平均曲率浸入的椭圆PDE系统与椭圆Liouville方程等价，给出了具体的等价映射。

Conclusion: 双曲3空间中的常平均曲率浸入问题可归约到椭圆Liouville方程，这为相关问题的研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We show, using group theoretic constructions, that the system of elliptic partial differential equations whose solutions determine the constant mean curvature immersions into hyperbolic 3 space are equivalent to the elliptic Liouville equation. In particular, the Darboux integrability of each equation shows that each of these equations admit an equivalent quotient representation which descends to an equivalence of the equations. The explicit map providing the equivalence is given.

</details>

### [95] [Quantum Markov Chains for an Asymmetric Mixed Ising-XY Model on a Cayley Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14343)
*Farrukh Mukhamedov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一类混合量子Ising-XY模型在Cayley树上的性质，证明了平移不变边界方程的唯一正解存在性，从而得到唯一的平移不变量子马尔可夫链，并分析了边界律动力学和两体纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在树结构上研究量子自旋模型，特别是混合相互作用类型（Ising和XY）的量子系统，探究其边界条件与量子马尔可夫链的存在唯一性。

Method: 利用树指标量子马尔可夫链的相容性准则，结合归一化迹，推导平移不变边界方程，显式计算局部转移算子（单步部分迹映射），分析边界律参数的有限维递归动力学。

Result: 证明了对于所有J_I, J_XY ∈ ℝ 和 β>0，边界方程存在唯一的正平移不变解；模型存在唯一的由正平移不变边界条件生成的平移不变量子马尔可夫链；边界律动力学无大于1的允许周期点；计算了自然三团簇上的局部两体位点纠缠。

Conclusion: 该混合量子Ising-XY模型在二阶Cayley树上具有良好定义的唯一平移不变量子马尔可夫链，边界动力学行为简单（无高阶周期点），且可解析计算局部纠缠性质。

Abstract: We study a mixed quantum Ising-$XY$ model on the semi-infinite rooted Cayley tree of order two. For every vertex $u$, the edge $\langle u,(u,1)\rangle$ carries an $XY$ interaction and the edge $\langle u,(u,2)\rangle$ carries an Ising interaction. Using the compatibility criterion for tree-indexed quantum Markov chains and consistently working with the normalized trace, we derive the translation-invariant boundary equation and compute explicitly the associated local transfer operator, namely the one-step partial-trace map which propagates successor boundary data to the parent vertex. We prove that the boundary equation has a unique positive translation-invariant solution for all $J_I,J_{XY}\in\mathbb R$ and $β>0$. Hence the model admits a unique translation-invariant quantum Markov chain generated by a positive translation-invariant boundary condition. We also show that the reduced boundary-law dynamics, i.e. the induced finite-dimensional recursion for the boundary-law parameters, has no admissible periodic points of period greater than one and compute the local two-site entanglement on the natural three-site cluster of the tree.

</details>


### [96] [On Factorizing Aggregate Counting Distributions into Independent Latent Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14409)
*Israel Klich*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了给定聚合计数变量的概率分布时，哪些独立潜在计数过程与之兼容。通过概率生成函数的正因式分解理论，引入正因式分解偏序集和因式分解熵，证明了尖锐熵不等式，刻画了等式成立条件，并建立了支持集障碍、实根与Hurwitz稳定区域等结果，确定了二、三次可因式分解区域，给出了四次Hurwitz体积的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在仅观察到聚合计数变量的概率分布时，如何判断哪些独立潜在计数过程与该观察兼容，即概率生成函数能否分解为具有非负系数的归一化多项式乘积。

Method: 发展正因式分解的数学理论，定义正因式分解偏序集（按细化排序）和因式分解熵（衡量与观测分布兼容的最大潜在香农熵）。通过熵不等式、支持集障碍、实根与Hurwitz稳定特征、局部稳定性定理等工具进行分析，结合精确计算和蒙特卡洛实验研究概率单纯形内的可因式分解区域几何。

Result: 证明了尖锐熵不等式并刻画了等式成立条件（潜在加和映射的单射性）；表明熵优化可限制在最大原子化分解上；发现不同最大原子化分解具有不同熵的例子；建立了基于支持集的可因式分解障碍；刻画了实根与Hurwitz稳定扇区；证明了互质因式分解的局部稳定性定理；精确确定了二、三次的可因式分解区域；得到了四次Hurwitz体积的精确值。

Conclusion: 正因式分解偏序集是概率生成函数的一个自然代数对象，为研究聚合计数统计中的潜在独立结构提供了框架。

Abstract: Given only the probability distribution of an aggregate counting variable, what independent latent counting processes are compatible with the observation? Equivalently, when does a probability-generating function admit a factorization into normalized polynomials with nonnegative coefficients? We develop a mathematical theory of such positive factorizations.
  We introduce the positive factorization poset, whose elements are all positive factorizations ordered by refinement, and define the factorization entropy, measuring the maximal latent Shannon entropy compatible with the observed distribution. We prove a sharp entropy inequality, characterize the equality case by injectivity of the latent addition map, show that entropy optimization may be restricted to maximal atomizations, and exhibit examples where distinct maximal atomizations have different entropy.
  We further establish support-based obstructions to positive factorization, characterize the real-rooted and Hurwitz-stable sectors, prove a local stability theorem for coprime factorizations, determine exactly the factorable regions in degrees two and three, obtain an exact quartic Hurwitz volume, and investigate the geometry of the factorable region inside the probability simplex through exact calculations and Monte Carlo experiments. These results identify the positive factorization poset as a natural algebraic object associated with probability-generating functions and provide a framework for studying latent independent structure in aggregate counting statistics.

</details>


### [97] [Polar Form of the Dirac Operator in Low-Dimensional Space-Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14752)
*Luca Fabbri,Giuseppe De Maria*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过极坐标形式将Dirac算子表达为指定矩阵的左乘，并利用该技术求解低维空间中的右预解式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种新的数学表达方式，以便更有效地处理Dirac算子在低维空间中的预解式问题。

Method: 采用极坐标形式（polar form）将Dirac算子重新表达为某一指定矩阵的左乘运算，并基于此方法推导右预解式。

Result: 成功找到了低维空间中Dirac算子的右预解式。

Conclusion: 极坐标形式的左乘表示是一种有效的技术手段，可用于求解Dirac算子的右预解式。

Abstract: We express in polar form the Dirac operator as left-multiplication by an assigned matrix: we use this technique to find its right-resolvent for low-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [98] [A C++ implementation of the G-Scheme stiff ODE solver with multi-resolution sparse hash-table kernel lookup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14918)
*Riccardo Malpica Galassi,Mauro Valorani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了cpp-gscheme，一个生产级C++实现的G-Scheme框架，结合多分辨率哈希表实现刚性ODE系统的自适应积分。在33个n-庚烷自燃机理上，哈希表化C++ G-Scheme实现了100%核集检索成功率、零在线特征分解，速度相比CVODE提升2.7倍（56物种）到9.3倍（654物种），成本缩放约为N^1.4（CVODE为N^1.9）。精度评估表明求解器误差可忽略，主要误差来自骨架简化；80物种骨架机理在5%点火延迟目标下获得225倍加速。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-Scheme框架的计算成本主要由每时间步的Jacobian特征系统（核集）评估主导，前期工作提出了用预计算查找表替代在线核集计算的思路并给出了Python实现。本文旨在将该方法移植到生产级C++环境，保证与Python参考的行为等价性，并验证其在大型化学反应机理上的性能和精度优势。

Method: 采用多分辨率稀疏哈希表策略，将在线核集计算替换为从预计算查找表中检索；开发了从Python到C++的移植方法，保证缩放、分箱和哈希语义一致，实现了按位精确的检索钩子和完全原生的化学源项评估路径。

Result: 在33个n-庚烷自燃机理（56-459物种）及654物种详细机理上：哈希表化C++ G-Scheme检索100%核集、零在线特征分解；速度优于CVODE 2.7倍（56物种）至9.3倍（654物种）；成本缩放N^1.4对比CVODE的N^1.9；求解器误差可忽略，主要误差来自骨架简化；80物种骨架机理在5%点火延迟目标下实现225倍加速。

Conclusion: cpp-gscheme成功将G-Scheme的哈希表策略从Python原型移植到生产级C++实现，在保证行为等价性的前提下显著提升了大规模化学反应系统求解的计算效率，验证了该方法在工程实践中的可行性和优越性。软件、训练集构建器和基准驱动均已开源。

Abstract: The G-Scheme is an explicit, adaptive integration framework for stiff systems of ordinary differential equations that exploits a local time-scale decomposition provided by the eigensystem of the Jacobian (the kernel set). Its computational cost is dominated by the evaluation of a new kernel set at each time step. In a recent paper [R.Malpica Galassi and M.Valorani, Combust. Theory Model. 2025], we introduced a multi-resolution sparse hash-table strategy that replaces online kernel computation with retrieval from a precomputed lookup table, demonstrated in Python. Here we present cpp-gscheme, a production-grade C++ implementation of both the G-Scheme and the multi-resolution hash table, together with the porting methodology that guarantees behavioural equivalence with the Python reference: identical scaling, binning and hashing semantics, a bitwise-inert retrieval hook in the integrator, and a fully native evaluation path for the chemical source terms. On 33 n-heptane autoignition mechanisms spanning 56-459 species plus the detailed 654-species mechanism, the hash-tabulated C++ G-Scheme retrieves 100 percent of the kernel sets, performs zero online eigen decompositions, and outperforms the natively compiled CVODE solver by a factor increasing from 2.7x (56 species) to 9.3x (654 species), with observed cost scaling of approximately N power 1.4 versus CVODE's N power 1.9. A three-metric accuracy assessment (ignition delay, equilibrium state, and accumulated entropy-progress error) shows that the solver contributes negligibly to the overall error budget, which is dominated by skeletal reduction. Consequently, a 5 percent ignition-delay target is achieved by the 80-species skeletal mechanism with a 225x speed-up over CVODE on the detailed mechanism. The software, training-set builders, and benchmark drivers are released as open source.

</details>


### [99] [Quasi-Pfaffian Solutions to Integrable Systems via Sylvester-Moutard Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15013)
*Claire R Gilson,Chen Shu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种名为准普法夫式(quasiPfaffian)的数学结构，它类似于非交换代数中的准行列式(quasideterminant)。基于准普法夫式的Sylvester恒等式，提出了一种新的Sylvester-Moutard变换，可为Moutard可积系统（如Novikov-Veselov方程和二维sine-Gordon方程）生成新解。此外，还从准普法夫式角度回顾了经典Moutard变换并讨论了相关性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非交换代数环境中，行列式不能直接使用，因此需要类似准行列式的结构。作者希望引入准普法夫式作为对应普法夫式的非交换推广，并基于其代数性质发展出新的变换方法，以生成可积系统的新解。

Method: 首先定义并构建了准普法夫式的数学框架；然后推导了准普法夫式的Sylvester恒等式；基于该恒等式提出Sylvester-Moutard变换；并将该变换应用于Novikov-Veselov方程和二维sine-Gordon方程以生成新解；最后从准普法夫式视角重新审视了经典Moutard变换。

Result: 成功引入了准普法夫式结构并建立了其Sylvester恒等式；基于此提出了Sylvester-Moutard变换；验证了该变换能够为Moutard可变换可积系统生成新解。

Conclusion: 准普法夫式是一种有效的非交换代数工具，其衍生的Sylvester-Moutard变换为求解相关可积系统提供了新途径，且该结构具有进一步研究的价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a mathematical structure called the quasiPfaffian. The quasiPfaffian is analogous to the quasideterminant, a structure used instead of a determinant in noncommutative settings. Building on the Sylvester identity for the quasiPfaffian, we develop a novel transformation, named the Sylvester Moutard transform, this generates new solutions for Moutard transformable integrable systems, such as the Novikov Veselov equation and the two dimensional sine Gordon equation. We also briefly review the classical Moutard transformation in the context of quasiPfaffians and discuss several additional properties of this new object.

</details>


### [100] [Study of Duffing oscillator using an improved Lindstedt Poincare method and relevant comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15233)
*Ramij Ahamed,Subhankar Ray*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用Lindstedt-Poincaré方法及其改进形式对无阻尼Duffing振荡器进行综合分析，并与高阶Runge-Kutta数值解对比，证明所提方法在收敛性上优于标准LPM和Burton改进的LPM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无阻尼Duffing振荡器是一类重要的非线性动力系统，现有标准LPM及其Burton改进在收敛性方面仍有不足，因此需要发展更优的解析方法以提升逼近精度。

Method: 采用Lindstedt-Poincaré方法及其改进形式进行摄动分析，将结果与高阶Runge-Kutta数值解进行对比，并通过收敛性比较评估标准LPM、Burton改进LPM与本文方法的优劣。

Result: 本文所提出的改进方法在收敛性能上优于标准LPM和Burton改进的LPM，与数值解吻合更好。

Conclusion: 本文提出的改进LPM方法能更有效地逼近无阻尼Duffing振荡器的真实解，具有更好的收敛性能。

Abstract: The undamped Duffing oscillator is a nonlinear dynamical system with broad applications in physics, engineering and biological system. We present a comprehensive analysis of this system using the Lindstedt Poincare method (LPM) and its modifications and make comparison with numerical solution obtained using higher order Runge-Kutta. It is also shown the method suggested in this article converges better than the standard LPM and Lindstedt Poincare method with Burton's modification.

</details>


### [101] ["Goldfish'' equations for infinitely many particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15237)
*Francois Leyvraz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了"金鱼方程"（一组可通过多项式系数与根变换求解的非线性常微分方程组）从有限N扩展到无穷N的可能性，主要难点在于从多项式到整函数的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维"金鱼方程"的精确解方法推广到无限维情形，探索其数学可行性。

Method: 通过将多项式系数与根的变换关系推广到整函数，尝试将无穷维金鱼方程转化为自由运动问题。

Result: 指出了从多项式到整函数的过渡是主要困难，未给出完整推广方案（非标准分析方法留待未来研究）。

Conclusion: 金鱼方程向无穷维推广存在根本性数学障碍（多项式→整函数），尚未找到等价变换路径。

Abstract: The ``goldfish'' equations, so named because of their striking beauty, are a system of $N$ nonlinear ODE's, where $N$ is an arbitrary integer. They can be solved exactly in a very simple manner, by transforming them to free motion using a transformation involving the transition from the set of {\em coefficients\/} of a polynomial to that of its {\em zeroes}. This paper aims to explore the possibility of extending this solution to the case in which $N$ is infinite. The main difficulty involves the transition from polynomials to entire functions. Another approach using non-standard analysis, is left to future work.

</details>


### [102] [A Geometric Derivation of the Einstein Equations from the Causal Action Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13871)
*Felix Finster,Christoph Krpoun*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了因果作用原理在光滑流形支撑测度下的几何结构，推导出该原理可导出洛伦兹度规，且欧拉-拉格朗日方程在正则化长度展开下满足爱因斯坦场方程，引力耦合常数等于正则化长度的平方，并提供了系统性的修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从因果费米子系统的因果作用原理出发，证明其变分极值问题在流形支撑下可自然地导出广义相对论的爱因斯坦方程，并揭示引力耦合常数的起源。

Method: 假设最小化测度的支撑为光滑流形$\tilde{M}$，引入振荡真空概念，通过拉格朗日量在$\tilde{M}$上诱导洛伦兹度规；分析因果作用的欧拉-拉格朗日方程，利用正则化长度进行幂级数展开，推导里奇张量满足的爱因斯坦方程。

Result: 拉格朗日量诱导出洛伦兹度规；欧拉-拉格朗日方程蕴含里奇张量满足爱因斯坦方程，能量动量张量由正则化长度的幂展开给出；引力耦合常数等于正则化长度的平方。

Conclusion: 因果作用原理在流形支撑下可推导出广义相对论的爱因斯坦方程，且该框架为修正引力理论提供了系统化的方法；同时给出了因果变分原理的一般几何结构（包括任意维度的联络、黎曼度规和曲率），而洛伦兹情形仅适用于四维因果费米子系统。

Abstract: The causal action principle for causal fermion systems is analyzed for a minimizing measure whose support is assumed to have the structure of a smooth manifold $\tilde{M}$. The concept of osculating vacua is introduced. It is shown that the Lagrangian induces on $\tilde{M}$ a Lorentzian metric. Moreover, the Euler-Lagrange equations of the causal action imply that the Ricci tensor must satisfy the Einstein equations of general relativity for an energy momentum tensor given in terms of a power expansion in the regularization length. The gravitational coupling constant is found to be the square of the regularization length. Our methods provide a systematic procedure for deriving corrections to the Einstein equations.
  The paper includes a self-contained introduction to causal variational principles and the causal action principle. Most geometric structures (connection, Riemannian metric and curvature) are introduced and analyzed in the general setting of causal variational principles for an arbitrary dimension of $\tilde{M}$. The Lorentzian setting works only for causal fermion systems and is worked out only in four spacetime dimensions.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

### [103] [Quantum many-body mixed phase space revealed by hybrid feedback control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14223)
*Hang Dong,Jie Ren,Andrew Hallam,Han Wang,Zhengyi Cui,Yiren Zou,Junlin Wang,Hekang Li,Qiujiang Guo,Zhen Wang,Lei Ying,Zlatko Papic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过混合量子-经典反馈协议，在超导量子处理器上自主发现并稳定了长寿命的规则轨迹，揭示了量子多体混合相空间的存在，为不可积量子系统的相干动力学控制建立了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复杂系统在有序与混沌之间的相变是非平衡物理学的核心挑战。经典可积系统在弱扰动下呈现规则与混沌轨迹共存的混合相空间，但这一行为在相互作用量子多体系统中长期难以实现。

Method: 开发并实验实现了一种混合量子-经典反馈协议：每次迭代将短时间量子演化与经典优化相结合，将动力学投影回低纠缠变分流形，从而从混沌演化中提取相干性，自主发现并稳定长寿命规则轨迹。

Result: 稳定的轨迹揭示了量子多体混合相空间的存在，该相空间源于非线性变分动力学，没有直接的经典或少体量子系统类比。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一个通用框架，可用于算法发现和控制此前实验无法触及的相干动力学。

Abstract: Understanding how complex systems transition between order and chaos is a central challenge of nonequilibrium physics. While weak perturbations of classical integrable systems give rise to a mixed phase space of coexisting regular and chaotic trajectories, analogous behavior in interacting quantum many-body systems has remained elusive. Here we develop and experimentally implement a hybrid quantum-classical feedback protocol that autonomously discovers and stabilizes long-lived regular trajectories in a superconducting quantum processor. Each iteration combines short-time quantum evolution with classical optimization that projects the dynamics back onto a low-entanglement variational manifold, effectively distilling coherence from chaotic evolution. The stabilized trajectories reveal a quantum many-body mixed phase space emerging from nonlinear variational dynamics, without a direct analogue in classical or few-body quantum systems. Our results establish a versatile framework for algorithmic discovery and control of coherent dynamics previously inaccessible to experiment.

</details>


### [104] [Transducer leakage error suppression using invariant-based shortcut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14175)
*Shi-Qiang Qiao,Zheng Shan,Xue-Ke Song,Shi-Lei Su,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于最优不变量捷径理论，提出了一种抑制自旋-超导混合量子系统中换能器泄漏误差的方法，通过虚拟光子作为换能器实现自旋量子比特与transmon量子比特间的能量交换，实现了99%以上的保真度并将泄漏概率抑制至0.01以下，优于传统π脉冲、DRAG、逆绝热捷径和L-BFGS-GRAPE等方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子系统中换能器泄漏误差会破坏量子计算的计算子空间，导致保真度下降，需要提出有效方法抑制泄漏误差以实现高保真量子操作。

Method: 采用最优不变量捷径理论结合不变量逆向工程设计优化脉冲控制，利用虚拟光子作为换能器连接自旋量子比特和transmon量子比特，通过精心设计的脉冲抑制从计算子空间到非计算子空间的泄漏。

Result: 最终态布居保真度在泄漏误差影响下仍可达99%以上，泄漏概率最低抑制至0.01；实现了计算子空间内高保真iSWAP门操作和纠缠态制备；在存在退相干和控制误差时保真度仍达99%，优于传统方案。

Conclusion: 该优化捷径方案能有效抵抗实际情况下泄漏误差和控制误差的影响，为混合量子系统的量子态精确操控提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a method for suppressing transducer leakage errors in spin-superconducting hybrid quantum systems with the theory of optimal invariant-based shortcut. By mediated virtual photons as a transducer to exchange the energy between the spin qubit and a transmon qubit, the fidelity of the population of the final state features a broad range
  above 99\% under the influence of leakage error. The leakage probability from computational subspace to non-computational subspace can be effectively suppressed at a lowest value with $0.01$.
  Based on the optimized pulse control designed by the invariant-based inverse engineering, the high-fidelity quantum iSWAP gate operations and entanglement state preparation within the computational subspace are achieved. {Compared to the traditional $π$ pulse, derivative removal by adiabatic gate, counter-diabatic shortcut schemes, and limited-memory Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno gradient ascent pulse engineering, the optimized shortcut scheme can still achieve a high fidelity with 99\% in the presence of decoherence and control error.} When taking into account the possibility of leakage errors in actual situations, our solution can still largely resist the influence of control errors. The results provides a feasible path for precisely manipulating the quantum state of hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking quantum simulation at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14212)
*Jeremy Hartse,Mohsin Raza,Shravan Shravan,Ivan H. Deutsch,Niklas Mueller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳定子疤痕的非平衡量子模拟可扩展验证方案，利用其可经典模拟和高效保真度估计的特性，通过有物理动机的误差模型证明了对这些态的模拟保真度可以约束经典难解模拟的保真度，从而为非平衡动力学中的量子优势实验提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机相较于经典计算机的优势应用仍在探索中，且对量子优势进行基准测试具有挑战性，需要一种可扩展的验证方案来评估非平衡量子模拟中的量子优势实验。

Method: 提出基于稳定子疤痕（stabilizer scars）的可扩展验证方案。稳定子疤痕是一类特殊的量子多体疤痕，其结构同时保证了经典可模拟性和高效的直接保真度估计。通过假设物理上合理的误差模型，证明量子模拟这些态的保真度可以约束经典难解模拟的保真度。

Result: 证明了在物理动机误差模型下，稳定子疤痕状态的量子模拟保真度能够作为经典难解模拟保真度的上界，为非平衡动力学中的量子优势实验提供了可操作的基准测试方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可扩展的非平衡量子模拟验证框架，利用稳定子疤痕的特性为量子优势实验提供了可靠的基准测试方案，有助于识别和评估量子计算机在非平衡动力学中的实际优势。

Abstract: The applications for which quantum computers will clearly outperform classical computers are still being identified and benchmarking such an advantage is challenging. We propose a scalable verification scheme for non-equilibrium quantum simulation based on stabilizer scars, a special class of quantum many-body scars, whose structure ensures both classical simulability and efficient direct fidelity estimation. Assuming a physically motivated error model, we show that the fidelity of quantum simulating these states bounds the fidelity of classically intractable simulations, providing a benchmark for quantum-advantage experiments in non-equilibrium dynamics.

</details>


### [106] [Magic Without Entanglement: Exact Revivals and Their Fisher Information Origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14222)
*Rishabh Jha,Karun Gadge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现魔法（magic）与纠缠（entanglement）是独立量子资源，并通过稳定子Rényi熵的曲率与量子Fisher信息之间的桥梁，以及Clifford剪枝电路对伊辛演化的图论构造，揭示了二者在多体动力学中的结构关系，证明在热力学极限下存在有限魔法密度与零纠缠密度的态族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔法与纠缠作为独立的量子资源，二者在多体动力学中的确切关系尚未厘清，本文旨在揭示它们之间的结构原理。

Method: 利用稳定子Rényi熵的二阶曲率与量子Fisher信息的归一化等价关系建立桥梁；通过Clifford剪枝电路对任意森林图上的对易伊辛演化进行构造，得到稳定子-Rényi熵族；在伊辛淬火和 kicked Floquet 链两种一维模型中精确求解。

Result: 证明了有限魔法密度与零纠缠密度在热力学极限下的共存；发现魔法与纠缠具有不同的复兴周期；识别出Clifford点处零魔法但有限二分纠缠；通过切空间几何确定了初始增长、扰动复兴提升及热力学魔法最小值的稳定性；大规模Pauli基矩阵乘积态计算验证了所有预言。

Conclusion: 魔法与纠缠是独立且可分离的量子资源，可通过量子Fisher信息测量实验检测魔法。

Abstract: Magic and entanglement are independent quantum resources, yet their exact relation in many-body dynamics has remained elusive. We uncover two structural principles. First, at any stabilizer state, the curvature of the second stabilizer Rényi entropy under an arbitrary Hermitian generator equals the quantum Fisher information up to a fixed normalization, creating a bidirectional bridge between computational and metrological resources. Second, for commuting Ising evolution on any forest graph, a Clifford pruning circuit yields the full stabilizer-Rényi family at arbitrary size and in any spatial embedding, thereby furnishing a graph-theoretic construction of families with finite magic density and vanishing entanglement density in the thermodynamic limit. We solve two paradigmatic one-dimensional realizations central to quantum simulation -- an Ising quench and a kicked Floquet chain -- exactly for arbitrary system size and directly in the thermodynamic limit, revealing finite magic density with vanishing entanglement density, distinct magic and entanglement revival periods, and Clifford points with zero magic but finite bipartite entanglement. The same tangent geometry fixes initial growth, perturbative revival lifting, and stability of thermodynamic magic minima. Large-scale Pauli-basis matrix-product-state calculations verify all predictions, and the tangent bridge yields a concrete protocol for detecting magic through established quantum-Fisher-information measurements.

</details>


### [107] [Analytic gradients for low-rank quantum optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14217)
*Leo Goutte,Vincenzo Savona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出低秩最优控制(LROC)方法，利用量子协议保持纯度的特性，通过低秩分解高效模拟开放量子系统，实现二次方时间与内存优化，在超导电路任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的脉冲优化面临密度矩阵全模拟计算成本过高的问题，现有梯度方法受限于系统规模，亟需一种可扩展的高效方法。

Method: LROC利用量子协议保持纯度的特性，对密度矩阵进行低秩分解，仅传播分解后的因子化形式，并推导相应的伴随方程以计算目标函数梯度，实现与模拟同数量级的梯度计算成本。

Result: 在五个超导电路任务（五量子比特GHZ态制备、CNOT门、量子比特读取、纠错基元）中验证，每个任务均达到与固有耗散极限一致的保真度。

Conclusion: LROC将脉冲级优化扩展到现有梯度方法无法企及的系统规模，为大规模开放量子系统的控制设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce low-rank optimal control (LROC), a method for designing control pulses in open quantum systems whose full density-matrix simulation is prohibitively expensive. The method exploits a feature of quantum computing itself: because protocols are designed to preserve purity, the density matrix is dominated by a few pure states and admits an accurate low-rank factorization. LROC propagates only this factorized form and, by deriving the corresponding adjoint equation, obtains the gradient of any differentiable objective at the same reduced cost as the simulation, leading to a quadratic improvement in time and memory compared to the full master equation. We illustrate the breadth of the method on four superconducting-circuit tasks: preparation of a five-qubit GHZ state, a CNOT gate, qubit readout, and an error correction primitive, modeled with realistic multilevel transmons, decay, and strong drives, in each case reaching fidelities consistent with the intrinsic dissipation limits. LROC thereby extends pulse-level optimization to system sizes beyond the reach of existing gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [108] [Nonplanar qubit with tunable gauge symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14229)
*Muqing Yu,Han Bi,Hengli Lo,Vishvesha Sridhar,Guilherme Delfino,Dmitry Green,Claudio Chamon,Nadya Mason,Andrew P. Higginbotham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在电路量子电动力学中引入非平面约瑟夫森连接，通过3×3交叉约瑟夫森阵列实现通量可调的Z₃组合规范对称性，实验能谱与神经网络变分量子态预测高度吻合，展示了高维量子系统的可预测性，并开创了利用电路QED工具探索晶格规范理论的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路量子电动力学中的约瑟夫森连接均为平面结构，本质上局限于低维度。作者旨在突破这一限制，探索非平面约瑟夫森连接实现的高维量子系统，并研究其是否能够展现组合规范对称性及自旋液体行为。

Method: 实验上构建了一个3×3交叉约瑟夫森阵列（非平面量子比特），利用超导腔进行光谱测量；理论上采用经过训练的神经网络生成变分量子态来预测激发谱，并与实验数据对比验证。

Result: 观测到的激发谱与神经网络预测高度吻合；在组合规范对称点附近观察到精细结构分裂，归因于弱隧穿或实验缺陷导致的对称性破缺；利用超导腔可外部诱导对称性破缺，并在组合规范对称点观察到对称性恢复，此时基态仅相差一个Z₃相位。

Conclusion: 非平面约瑟夫森连接可产生通量可调的Z₃组合规范对称性，有望在晶格网络中实现自旋液体行为；该工作开创了利用电路QED工具箱探索晶格规范理论的新领域，并为任意维度与几何结构的量子系统研究打开了广阔空间。

Abstract: Circuit quantum electrodynamics embeds Josephson junction qubits within superconducting cavities, and has emerged as a leading approach to quantum computing and quantum simulation. Despite the many permutations of circuit geometry that have been explored, Josephson connectivities have so far been planar, making them effectively low-dimensional. Here we show that a non-planar qubit -- a $3\times3$ crossbar Josephson array -- gives rise to flux-tunable $\mathbb{Z}_3$ combinatorial gauge symmetry (CGS), potentially enabling spin-liquid behavior when networked into a lattice. The observed excitation spectrum shows excellent agreement with predictions from a neural network trained to generate variational quantum states, demonstrating that we have predictive power over our high-dimensional quantum system. Fine-structure splittings near the CGS point are compatible with weak tunneling or symmetry breaking due to experimental imperfections. We additionally use the superconducting cavity to externally induce symmetry breaking, observing a restoration of symmetry at the CGS point where ground states differ only by a $\mathbb{Z}_3$ phase. This work initiates a general program exploring lattice gauge theories using the toolbox of circuit quantum electrodynamics. More broadly, introducing non-planar Josephson connectivities opens a vast space for experimental and theoretical exploration of structures in almost any imaginable dimensionality and geometry.

</details>


### [109] [Disorder-enhanced compressibility of Floquet random quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14296)
*Francesca De Franco,Dante M. Kennes,David J. Luitz,Matteo Rizzi,Markus Schmitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了量子电路中时间演化算符的可压缩性与多体系统动力学 regime 的关系，发现强无序（局域化）系统比弱无序（混沌）系统更容易压缩，这有利于在噪声量子设备上进行压缩量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件受限于噪声和退相干，高保真度实现的酉电路深度受限，因此需要探究哪些动力学 regime 下时间演化算符更容易被压缩，以适配浅层电路。

Method: 以一维 Floquet 随机电路为测试平台，调控相互作用与无序的竞争；利用张量网络模拟计算 Floquet 酉算符的算子纠缠熵以及 out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) 来刻画算子增长；通过优化浅层砖墙电路来逼近 Floquet 演化。

Result: 弱无序下算子快速 scrambling，强无序下 OTOC 前沿传播缓慢且算子纠缠熵呈对数或近对数增长；在固定对数保真度密度下，强无序电路可压缩至比弱无序电路小得多的深度。

Conclusion: 局域化或缓慢 scrambling 的动力学为噪声设备上的压缩量子模拟提供了有利的 regime。

Abstract: Current quantum hardware is limited by noise and decoherence, which restrict the depth of unitary circuits that can be implemented with high fidelity. We investigate how the compressibility of time-evolution operators depends on the dynamical regime of the underlying many-body system. As a testbed, we study a one-dimensional Floquet random circuit with a tunable competition between interactions and on-site disorder. Using tensor-network simulations, we characterize operator growth through the operator-entanglement entropy of the Floquet unitary as well as of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We find rapid operator scrambling at weak disorder, while strong disorder leads to slow OTOC-front propagation and logarithmic or near-logarithmic operator-entanglement growth over the accessible time window. We then optimize shallow brickwall circuits to approximate the Floquet evolution and show that strong-disorder circuits can be compressed to substantially smaller depths than weak-disorder circuits at fixed logarithmic fidelity density. These results suggest that localized or slowly scrambling dynamics provide a favorable regime for compressed quantum simulation on noisy devices.

</details>


### [110] [Principles of Quantum Optimization for Constrained Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14227)
*Einar Gabbassov,Gurpahul Singh,Achim Kempf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一类谱理论来统一多种量子优化算法，发现计算速度下降由纠缠重构（纠缠的创建、重新分布和破坏）驱动，约束的代数性质会诱发这种重构，而约束感知动力学通过避免不必要的重构来缓解速度下降，从而解释了为何约束感知量子方法优于通用惩罚方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束组合优化是许多工业和科技决策问题的核心，但现有量子优化算法缺乏统一的理论框架来解释不同方法之间的性能差异，尤其是约束感知方法为何优于通用惩罚方法。

Method: 发展了一套谱理论来统一连续时间与基于电路的量子优化范式，通过分析纠缠重构（熵的创建、重新分布和破坏）与系统演化的关系，研究约束的代数性质如何影响计算速度下降。

Result: 计算速度下降由纠缠重构驱动，其严重程度取决于需要改变多少纠缠；约束的代数性质会诱发这种重构，约束感知动力学通过避免不必要的重构来减少速度下降。

Conclusion: 该理论成功解释了约束感知量子方法为何能优于通用惩罚方法，并将约束优化、计算复杂性、纠缠动力学和哈密顿量谱结构联系在一个统一框架中。

Abstract: Constrained combinatorial optimization underlies many industrial and technological decision problems. We develop a spectral theory that unifies many quantum optimization algorithms. We show that computational slowdown is driven by entanglement restructuring: the creation, redistribution, and destruction of entanglement during system evolution. The severity of the slowdown depends on how much entanglement must be changed. We show that algebraic properties of constraints induce such restructuring, and that constraint-aware dynamics reduce the associated slowdown by avoiding unnecessary restructuring. This framework explains why constraint-aware quantum methods can outperform generic penalty-based approaches. The theory connects constrained optimization, computational complexity, entanglement dynamics, and Hamiltonian spectral structure across continuous-time and circuit-based quantum optimization paradigms.

</details>


### [111] [Circuit complexity lower bounds for quantum spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14384)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子p-自旋玻璃模型中制备近基态所需的电路深度，证明当平均相互作用度随n增长时，对于足够大的固定p，任何制备能量接近最优值的n量子比特态都需要Ω_p(log n)深度；在有限平均度区域，存在固定深度阻碍。这为随机量子自旋玻璃提供了类似"No Low-Energy Trivial States"问题的阻碍结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论中的一个核心问题是标准多体模型所产生态的电路复杂度。Anschuetz等人证明量子p-自旋玻璃的最优能量与乘积态能达到的最佳能量之间存在间隙，但浅层电路能否填补这一间隙尚不清楚，因为即使是深度为1的电路也能产生纠缠。

Method: 利用高斯过程在浅层电路上的均匀控制，将随机量子哈密顿量的态制备下界问题转化为概率论问题。通过分析平均相互作用度与电路深度的关系，分别处理了平均度随n增长和有限平均度两种情形。

Result: 1) 当平均相互作用度随n增长时，对于足够大的固定p，任何制备能量在最优值的固定正常数范围内的n量子比特态都需要Ω_p(log n)深度。2) 在有限平均度区域，对于任意固定深度D，足够大的度前置因子会阻止深度D制备近基态。两个结果对任意数量的辅助量子比特均成立。

Conclusion: 研究结果为随机量子自旋玻璃提供了类似NLTS问题的阻碍结果，开辟了通过概率论方法研究NLTS-like问题的途径，与基于编码的哈密顿量（如Anshu等人的工作，其基态存在多项式规模的制备电路）形成对比。

Abstract: A central question in quantum information theory is the circuit complexity of states arising from standard many-body models. We study this question for quantum $p$-spin glasses, random Hamiltonians whose interactions act on $p$-tuples of qubits through Pauli strings. Anschuetz, Gamarnik, and Kiani (arXiv:2404.07231) showed that the optimum energy is separated from the best energy achievable by product states. This leaves open whether shallow circuits can close the gap, since even depth-one circuits can generate entanglement.
  We show that the entanglement needed to close the product-state gap cannot be generated at shallow depth. When the average interaction degree grows with $n$, we prove that, for all sufficiently large fixed $p$, any circuit preparing an $n$-qubit state whose normalized energy is within a fixed positive constant of the optimum must have depth $Ω_p(\log n)$. In the bounded-average-degree regime, we prove a fixed-depth obstruction: for every fixed $D$, a sufficiently large degree prefactor rules out depth-$D$ preparation of near-ground states. Both results hold uniformly over circuits with an arbitrary number of ancilla qubits.
  Our results give an obstruction in the spirit of the No Low-Energy Trivial States problem of Freedman and Hastings (arXiv:1301.1363), but for random quantum spin glasses rather than code-based Hamiltonians such as those of Anshu, Breuckmann, and Nirkhe (arXiv:2206.13228), whose ground states admit polynomial-size preparation circuits. This setting opens a probabilistic route to NLTS-like questions: we recast state-preparation lower bounds for random quantum Hamiltonians as uniform control of Gaussian processes indexed by shallow circuits.

</details>


### [112] [Tuning the universality class of a quantum process by Trotterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14663)
*Robin Prampain dit Boulan,Jerom Jebai,Hendrik Weimer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值分析研究离散化量子接触过程，发现其相变的普适性类别依赖于离散化方式，并证明破坏性干涉效应是量子普适性类别偏离经典有向渗流类别的量子力学根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间版本量子接触过程被认为属于新的量子普适性类别，而相关实验却指向经典有向渗流类别。这种矛盾需要被澄清。

Method: 对离散化量子接触过程进行数值分析，构建系统性方法在连续和离散结果之间连续插值，研究破坏性干涉效应的影响。

Result: 离散化细节导致了相变普适性类别的差异；通过插值方法可统一不同结果；破坏性干涉效应是量子普适性类别偏离经典的根源。

Conclusion: 量子接触过程的普适性类别差异源于离散化方式，且其量子力学特性（破坏性干涉）对普适性类别有本质性影响。

Abstract: We numerically analyze a discretized version of the quantum contact process, a non-equilibrium model having a competition between a coherent infection process and a dissipative recovery process. Previously, the continuous-time version has been discussed to have a phase transition in a novel quantum universality class, while experimental investigations of a related process have pointed towards the transition being in the classical directed percolation class. We demonstrate that these differences stem from details of the discretization of the process and construct a systematic way to continuously interpolate between these results. Finally, we provide evidence that the modification of the universality class is due to destructive interference effects, showing a clear quantum-mechanical origin.

</details>


### [113] [A Compass on the Quantum State Sphere: The Hopf Ansatz for Arbitrary Pure-State Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14231)
*Ruge Lin,Guangxi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Hopf ansatz，一种基于二叉树的通用量子态制备与优化框架。该结构通过内部角控制子树概率、叶相位承载复自由度，并提供从振幅到物理角的逆映射。针对哈密顿量等目标，梯度分量可表示为当前态与切向态之间的跃迁矩，通过期望值测量实现。梯度编译电路族数量仅随希尔伯特空间维度对数增长。确定性实态基准测试表明，该优化器可达数值精度中位数差距，在VQE中表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意量子态向量的优化如同在希尔伯特空间单位球面上导航：除了目标可达性，还需要坐标、局部距离信息和可测量的方向。作者旨在建立一个可导航的通用纯态优化框架。

Method: 提出Hopf ansatz——一种二叉树枝干电路，用于任意归一化实数和复数态向量。内部角控制子树间概率，叶相位承载复自由度。同一棵树同时给出态制备和从振幅到物理角的显式逆映射。逆映射提供坐标，对角诱导度量提供局部距离信息，非零坐标切向量成为可制备的归一化态。对于哈密顿量等目标，每个梯度分量是当前态与切向态之间跃迁矩乘以已知比例因子。分支态构造通过期望值测量表达跃迁矩，树按幅度层和叶索引相位族组织编译梯度设置。梯度编译电路族数量随希尔伯特空间维度对数增长，测量预算作为独立统计成本。

Result: 在确定性实态基准测试中（使用精确成本、精确梯度和已知全局最优），度量感知的Hopf优化器达到数值精度中位数差距。最清晰的基线增益体现在：更小的VQE平均最终差距，以及更强的计量学启发迹在数值精度处的集中。

Conclusion: Hopf ansatz将通用态制备转化为可导航的任意纯态优化框架，为量子变分算法提供了高效梯度获取和优化的新途径。

Abstract: Optimizing arbitrary quantum state vectors is like navigating the unit sphere in Hilbert space: beyond target reachability, optimization asks for coordinates, local distance information, and measurable directions. We introduce the Hopf ansatz, a binary-tree circuit for arbitrary normalized real and complex state vectors. Internal angles steer probability between subtrees, leaf phases carry the complex degrees of freedom, and the same tree gives state preparation and an explicit inverse map from amplitudes to physical angles. Together these structures act as a compass for the search: the inverse map gives coordinates, the diagonal induced metric gives local distance information, and nonzero coordinate tangents become preparable normalized states.
  For Hamiltonian objectives, and for objectives with the same local transition-moment form, each gradient component is a known scale factor times a transition moment between the current state and a tangent state. A branch-state construction expresses these moments through expectation-value measurements, while the tree organizes the compiled gradient settings by magnitude layer and leaf-indexed phase family. Thus the number of distinct compiled gradient-access circuit families grows only logarithmically with Hilbert-space dimension, while the measurement budget required for a chosen precision remains a separate statistical cost. In deterministic real-state benchmarks with exact costs, exact gradients, and known global optima, metric-aware Hopf optimizers reach numerical-precision median gaps, with the clearest baseline gains in smaller VQE mean final gaps and stronger concentration of metrology-inspired traces at numerical precision. The Hopf ansatz turns universal state preparation into a navigable framework for arbitrary pure-state optimization.

</details>


### [114] [Electrons Hopping across a Molecular Network: Spectra and Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14687)
*Ludwig Schulz,Max Best,Carsten Henkel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究有限分子环网络中相互作用自旋无关电子的Hubbard模型，分析晶格几何、库仑相互作用和离散对称性对多体能谱和本征态的影响，特别关注网络对称性的作用以及偶数/奇数粒子系统的定性区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有限分子环网络中，晶格几何、库仑相互作用与离散对称性之间的相互影响，以及这些因素如何决定多体能谱结构和本征态的Mulliken分类。

Method: 采用紧束缚Hubbard哈密顿量描述相互作用自旋无关电子，使用群论方法分析不同尺寸环网络（L=3,4,5,6节点）在填充1、2或3个电子时的能谱演化，并引入外磁场研究塞曼效应。

Result: 识别出偶数与奇数粒子系统之间的定性区别；在半填充时，粒子-空穴对称性（对偶性）保护特定对称扇区免受塞曼分裂；塞曼效应结合库仑相互作用在对称扇区中产生避免交叉。

Conclusion: 网络对称性在决定多体能谱结构中起关键作用，偶奇粒子数系统存在本质差异，且粒子-空穴对称性为半填充系统提供保护机制。

Abstract: We investigate interacting spinless electrons on finite molecular ring networks described by a tight-binding Hubbard Hamiltonian. The interplay between lattice geometry, Coulomb interactions and discrete symmetries is analysed for rings with $L=3,4,5,6$ nodes, filled with one, two or three electrons. Special attention is devoted to the role of the network symmetries in determining the structure of the many-body spectrum and the Mulliken classification of the eigenstates.
