Using group-theoretical methods, we examine the evolution of the spectra in the presence of an external magnetic flux. The Zeeman effect lifts degeneracies and results in combination with the Coulomb interaction to avoided crossings in symmetry sectors. We identify a qualitatively distinction between systems with an even and odd number of particles. At half-filling, particle-hole symmetry (duality) protects selected symmetry sectors against Zeeman splitting.

</details>


### [115] [Fock-state preparation based on amplitude amplification in cavity QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14239)
*Sharoon Austin,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Kartik Srinivasan,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于振幅放大的腔QED相干控制技术，在两个物理平台上实现了改进：一是通过 oblivious 振幅放大将单光子生成协议的复杂度从 N~1/p 降低到 N~1/√p，并减少了腔损耗误差；二是在色散区域使用定点振幅放大制备Fock态，协议长度标度为 O(n^{1/4})，并扩展至NOON态的制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腔QED中单光子生成协议的成功概率受弱控制脉冲限制，需要重复多次（N~1/p），效率低下；同时，强控制脉冲会导致较大的腔损耗误差。制备Fock态的传统方法效率也不够高。因此需要一种更高效、误差更小的相干控制技术。

Method: (1) 针对三能级量子发射器耦合光学腔的单光子生成，提出了 oblivious 振幅放大的扩展方法，仅使用信号量子比特上的反射幺正算符以及 U 和 U† 操作。(2) 针对超导量子比特耦合微波腔的色散区域，使用定点振幅放大协议制备Fock态。

Result: (1) 单光子生成协议长度从 N~1/p 优化到 N~1/√p，且在多个实验相关区域降低了腔固有损耗引起的误差。(2) Fock态制备协议长度标度为 O(n^{1/4})，并成功推广到NOON态的制备。

Conclusion: 振幅放大技术在腔QED中实现了更高效的单光子生成、更低误差以及更优标度的Fock态制备，展示了该框架在量子信息处理中的广泛应用前景。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a coherent control technique for cavity QED based on amplitude amplification. We consider two physical platforms. In the first setting, we study a three-level quantum emitter coupled to a single mode of an optical cavity and introduce a protocol for producing traveling single photons based on oblivious amplitude amplification. As the key ingredient of our protocol, we propose an extension of oblivious amplitude amplification which uses reflection unitaries solely on the signal qubit, along with $U$ and $U^\dagger$, where the unitary $U$ prepares the initial state. Our approach improves the scaling of the single-photon-generation protocol length from $N\sim 1/p$ to $N\sim 1/\sqrt{p}$, with $p$ denoting the success probability of obtaining a short single photon from a single application of the weak control pulse. Furthermore, our protocol also reduces the error from intrinsic cavity loss compared to protocols using a single strong control pulse in various experimentally relevant regimes, suggesting the application of our methods for error reduction. In the second setting, we consider a superconducting qubit coupled to a single bosonic mode of a microwave cavity in a circuit QED architecture in the dispersive regime for preparing Fock states. Using fixed-point amplitude amplification, we obtain a protocol for preparing Fock states whose length scales as $O(n^{1/4})$, where $n$ is the number of photons. Additionally, as an application of our methods for state preparation, we describe a protocol for preparing NOON states.

</details>


### [116] [A Geometric Theory of Fermion-to-Qubit Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14883)
*Lakshya Nagpal,Nishith Reen,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明费米子到量子比特的映射不仅是量子模拟的算法工具，还定义了具有物理意义的几何表示。通过基于加权超图和耦合空间表示的几何框架，作者在Bravyi-Kitaev和Xia-Bian-Kais编码中发现了几何普适类、精确谱结构，并利用最优输运量化相互作用驱动的重组。应用于多个多体模型，证明超图几何能一致地刻画编码量子哈密顿量的结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上费米子到量子比特的变换被视为算法工具，但作者认为这些变换还定义了内在的几何表示，其结构编码了超越谱等价的物理信息。目的是揭示这些变换的几何本质，建立超图几何作为理解量子多体哈密顿量的新框架。

Method: 基于加权超图和耦合空间表示构建几何框架；在BK表示中引入几何可观测量比较动力学和相互作用超图的代数连通性，推导相互作用强度的解析依赖关系；在XBK表示中用概率测度和最优输运描述哈密顿量的演化；应用于Hubbard、tV、单杂质Anderson和Kitaev模型进行验证。

Result: 发现了两个几何普适类以及源于编码二叉树结构的精确谱组织；最优输运独立于谱分析量化了相互作用驱动的重组；四种多体模型的几何描述一致地捕捉了编码哈密顿量的结构演化。

Conclusion: 费米子到量子比特的编码不仅是计算映射，也是量子多体哈密顿量的几何表示。超图几何为理解编码及其物理内容提供了新框架。

Abstract: Exact fermion to qubit transformations are conventionally regarded as algorithmic tools that translate many-body Hamiltonians into qubit representations for quantum simulation. Here we show that they also define intrinsic geometric representations whose structure encodes physically meaningful information beyond spectral equivalence. We develop a geometric framework based on weighted hypergraphs and coupling space representations constructed from the Bravyi--Kitaev (BK) and Xia--Bian--Kais (XBK) encodings. Within the BK representation, we introduce a geometric observable that compares the algebraic connectivities of the kinetic and interaction hypergraphs, derive its exact analytical dependence on interaction strength, and uncover two geometric universality classes together with an exact spectral organization originating from the binary tree architecture of the encoding. The complementary XBK representation describes the evolution of encoded Hamiltonians through probability measures in coupling space, where optimal transport quantifies interaction-driven reorganization independently of the spectral analysis. Applications to the Hubbard, spinless tV , single impurity Anderson, and Kitaev models demonstrate that these connectivity and transport based geometric descriptions consistently capture the structural evolution of encoded quantum Hamiltonians across distinct classes of many-body systems. Our results establish hypergraph geometry as a new framework for understanding fermion-to-qubit encodings,revealing that they serve not only as computational mappings but also as geometric representations of quantum many-body Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [117] [Emulation of Entanglement Distribution Networks on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14260)
*Ashley N. Tittelbaugh,Jerry Horgan,Rohan Bali,Marco Ruffini,Daniel C. Kilper,Shelbi L. Jenkins,Boulat A. Bash*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用量子计算机模拟量子网络，并评估在实际缺陷（如退化的纠缠和通信延迟）下基于远距传态的分布式多体纠缠态构建性能。通过 depolarizing 噪声和热弛豫分别模拟不完美 Bell 对源和经典通信延迟，对比了 Stinespring dilation、随机 Pauli 错误和准概率分解三种实现方式在 IQM 量子硬件和仿真中的表现。尽管这些噪声模型数学等价，但硬件约束导致结果存在显著差异，强调了实验设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算机模拟量子网络的可行性，并研究实际硬件缺陷（如纠缠退化和通信延迟）对分布式纠缠态构建性能的影响，以便为未来的量子网络实验设计提供指导。

Method: 使用 depolarizing 噪声信道建模不完美 Bell 对源，热弛豫建模经典通信延迟；分别采用 Stinespring dilation、随机 Pauli 错误和准概率分解三种方式实现 depolarization，在 IQM 量子硬件和仿真平台上进行评估。

Result: 尽管三种噪声模型在数学上等价，但硬件约束导致它们在实验结果上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 在量子网络仿真中，不能仅依赖数学等价的噪声模型，必须考虑实际硬件约束，精心设计实验才能获得可靠的结果。

Abstract: We investigate how quantum computers can be used to emulate quantum networks and study their performance under practical impairments. In particular, we evaluate how degraded entanglement and communication latency affect teleportation-based distributed multipartite-entanglement-state construction. We model imperfect Bell-pair sources using depolarizing noise channels and classical communication delays using thermal relaxation. We implement the depolarization using Stinespring dilation, randomly applied Pauli errors, and quasi-probability decompositions, evaluating the latter two on IQM quantum hardware and all three in simulation. We then study the performance of the entanglement distribution under noise generated by the aforementioned models. Although these noise models are mathematically equivalent, we find that hardware constraints result in profound differences in the corresponding results, highlighting the importance of careful experiment design.

</details>


### [118] [Implicit differentiation of tensor network algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15030)
*Lander Burgelman,Anna Francuz,Paul Brehmer,Lukas Devos,Jutho Haegeman,Frank Verstraete,Bram Vanhecke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用隐式微分技术优化投影纠缠对态(PEPS)的变分优化，通过将梯度计算重构为单个特征方程，降低了计算成本并改善了规模缩放，同时利用收缩环境的内在对称性消除了数值不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于自动微分的PEPS变分优化方法存在两大问题：能量梯度计算是主要的计算瓶颈，且频繁出现数值不稳定性。需要更高效、更稳定的梯度计算方法。

Method: 将梯度计算的核心步骤重构为关于收缩环境的单一特征方程，并利用收缩环境的内在对称性选择合适参数化来消除子程序导数引起的不稳定性。

Result: 降低了梯度计算成本，改善了随问题规模的缩放；直接消除了全局梯度计算中的不稳定性；大幅简化了稳定梯度PEPS优化的实际实现。

Conclusion: 隐式微分技术为PEPS变分优化提供了一种更高效、更稳定且实现更简单的梯度计算方法，克服了自动微分方法的主要缺陷。

Abstract: The current leading approach to the variational optimization of projected entangled-pair states (PEPS) is based on automatic differentiation, which allows for a convenient evaluation of the energy gradient with respect to the local variational degrees of freedom. However, evaluating the energy gradient not only remains a major computational bottleneck of the optimization procedure, but also suffers from frequent numerical instabilities. In this work, we adopt recent advances in implicit differentiation techniques to address these challenges in PEPS optimization. By reformulating the core step of the gradient computation in terms of a single characteristic equation for the contraction environment, we reduce the cost of the gradient computation and improve its scaling with the problem size. By choosing a suitable parametrization of this characteristic equation based on the intrinsic symmetries of the contraction environment, we can directly remove instabilities from the global gradient computation that would otherwise arise from the derivatives of subroutines of the contraction algorithm. Finally, we demonstrate how this approach drastically simplifies the practical implementation of stable gradient-based PEPS optimization.

</details>


### [119] [Worldline-Susceptibility Scheduling for Quantum Annealing Beyond Local-Adiabatic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14282)
*Suraj Singh,Lakshya Nagpal,Vikas Chauhan,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于世界线磁化率（可在平衡态蒙特卡洛采样中低成本获取）的替代退火调度方案，无需谱隙先验知识。在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick自旋玻璃模型上，该方案不仅一致优于线性退火，还在相当比例实例中超越了理论上最优的Roland-Cerf局域绝热调度。究其原因，是精确局域绝热调度在有限时间下存在边界隙阱和振荡不稳定性两种失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火的性能高度依赖于退火时间在演化过程中的分配方式。Roland-Cerf局域绝热调度虽理论上最优，但需要瞬时谱隙的完整知识，对大规模优化问题不可行。因此需要一种计算廉价、不依赖谱信息的替代调度方案。

Method: 提出基于世界线磁化率（worldline magnetization susceptibility）的替代调度方案。该磁化率通过平衡态蒙特卡洛采样直接获得，无需谱信息即可识别退火过程中的临界区域。在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick自旋玻璃模型上，以精确对角化作为基准，对比线性调度、Roland-Cerf调度与所提方法的性能。

Result: 所提调度一致优于线性退火；在相当比例实例中甚至超越了精确的Roland-Cerf调度。进一步分析发现，Roland-Cerf调度在有限时间下存在两种失效模式：边界隙阱（最小谱隙出现在退火末端）和振荡不稳定性（时间过度集中在内部最小隙附近导致震荡）。

Conclusion: 基于廉价平衡态可观测量构建的鲁棒调度，在现实的有限时间条件下可以超越依赖精确谱隙的策略。完整方法已开源实现于Qanneal框架中。

Abstract: The performance of quantum annealing depends critically on how the available annealing time is distributed along the evolution. Although the Roland Cerf local adiabatic schedule is theoretically optimal, it requires complete knowledge of the instantaneous spectral gap, making it impractical for large optimization problems. We propose a computationally inexpensive surrogate schedule based on the worldline magnetization susceptibility measured during simulated quantum annealing. The susceptibility is obtained directly from equilibrium Monte Carlo sampling and identifies the critical region of the anneal without requiring spectral information. Using exact diagonalization of Sherrington Kirkpatrick spin glass instances as ground truth, we show that the resulting schedule consistently outperforms conventional linear annealing and, for a substantial fraction of instances, also surpasses the exact Roland Cerf schedule. We demonstrate that this unexpected behaviour originates from two finite time failure modes of exact local adiabatic scheduling a boundary gap trap, in which the minimum spectral gap occurs at the end of the anneal, and an oscillatory instability caused by excessively localized time allocation around an interior minimum gap. These results suggest that robust scheduling based on inexpensive equilibrium observables can outperform exact spectral gap based strategies under realistic finite time conditions. The complete methodology is implemented in the open source Qanneal framework.

</details>


### [120] [An experimental pathway towards an exact theory of strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15089)
*Eugenia Pyurbeeva,Ronnie Kosloff*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用GKLS主方程的数学等价形式，提出了一个强耦合二能级系统的精确理论描述，并设计了基于固态量子点器件的实验方案来揭示强耦合区域的基本参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合区域中描述开放量子系统的三个基本参数（精细平衡关系的类比、两个耦合强度常数）在弱耦合框架外是未知的，需要建立新的理论框架和实验手段来揭示这些参数，从而加深对强耦合物理的理解。

Method: 采用GKLS主方程的一种数学等价形式，建立强耦合二能级系统的精确理论；提出基于固态电子量子点器件的可行实验协议，通过测量揭示该理论框架中的三个未知参数。

Result: 该理论框架直观且与现有结果（如热化到非正则态）一致；提出了一个可行的实验方案来测量强耦合区域的基本参数。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论和实验方案能够揭示强耦合区域的基本参数，从而推动对强耦合物理的基本理解。

Abstract: We employ a mathematically equivalent form of the GKLS master equation to arrive at an exact theoretical description of a two-level system strongly coupled to the environment. The framework, while intuitive, shedding light on the physics of the problem, and agreeing with existing results, such as thermalisation to a non-canonical state, is based around three parameters that are unknown outside of the weak coupling regime -- the analogue to the detailed balance relation, and two coupling strength constants. As a way forward, we propose a feasible experimental protocol based on a solid-state electronic quantum dot device, through which the fundamental parameters of the problem can be revealed, which would further the fundamental understanding of strong coupling.

</details>


### [121] [Efficient quantum algorithm for Heisenberg spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14401)
*Sergey Bravyi,David Gosset,Yinchen Liu,Benjamin Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由Suzuki和Fisher在1971年研究的Heisenberg型量子自旋系统（包括任何二分图上的Heisenberg反铁磁体）。这些模型的基态和Gibbs态是半径为1的Lee-Yang张量，对应具有单位多圆盘内零自由性质的多元多项式。对于该族中的每个哈密顿量，证明了第一激发态与基态能量之间的能隙下界为2μ（μ为Z方向磁场）。基于此结果，获得了该族任意模型基态能量的高效量子绝热算法。证明基于一个新不等式，将半正定算子的能隙与其Lee-Yang半径联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找Heisenberg型量子自旋系统基态能量的高效量子算法，利用Lee-Yang理论中的零自由性质来建立能隙下界。

Method: 利用Lee-Yang张量理论，建立半正定算子谱间隙与其Lee-Yang半径之间的新不等式关系，从而导出能隙下界。

Result: 证明了该族哈密顿量的谱间隙下界为2μ（Z方向磁场），并据此设计了高效的量子绝热算法。

Conclusion: Lee-Yang张量的零自由性质可用于推导量子自旋系统的能隙下界，并构造高效的绝热量子算法。

Abstract: We consider a broad family of Heisenberg-type quantum spin systems that were studied by Suzuki and Fisher in 1971. This family includes, for example, the Heisenberg antiferromagnet on any bipartite graph. The ground and Gibbs states of these models are Lee-Yang tensors with radius 1: they are associated with multilinear polynomials that possess an extraordinary zero-freeness property inside the unit polydisk in the complex plane. For each Hamiltonian in this family we show that the spectral gap between the first-excited and ground-state energies is lower bounded by $2μ$, where $μ$ is the magnetic field along the $Z$ direction. Using this result we obtain an efficient quantum adiabatic algorithm for the ground energy of any model in this family. The proof is based on a new inequality that relates the spectral gap of a positive semidefinite operator to its Lee-Yang radius -- a quantitative strengthening of prior work of the authors that may find applications elsewhere.

</details>


### [122] [Real-Time Detection of Charge Jumps in Superconducting Qubits with a Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14293)
*Daniel Gaytan-Villarreal,Peter Meiring,Daniel Baxter,Daniel Bowring,Grace Bratrud,Matteo Cremonesi,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Grace Wagner,Bowen Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩张因果卷积神经网络(DCCNN)的在线电荷跳变检测器，用于超导量子比特，该检测器在FPGA上以6.19μs延迟运行，在匹配虚警率下达到与离线χ²算法相当的检测效率(0.843 vs 0.866)，无需逐量子比特超参数调优，将电荷跳变检测从后诊断工具转变为控制环路原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离辐射导致的电荷跳变会引发超导量子比特的相关错误，挑战容错量子计算，同时也可用于量子传感。现有检测方法离线运行，引入的延迟不适合在环量子比特控制。

Method: 使用扩张因果卷积神经网络(DCCNN)，基于从Fermilab NEXUS站点测量的量子比特模板生成的合成Ramsey层析扫描数据训练，通过hls4ml将模型翻译为FPGA固件，采用ap_fixed<16,6>量化，部署在Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216上。

Result: DCCNN单次推理延迟为6.19μs，检测效率为0.843±0.022，离线χ²算法为0.866±0.020（在|Δq|∈[0.1, 0.5]e区间，匹配虚警率条件下），且无需每量子比特超参数调优。

Conclusion: 该方法将电荷跳变检测从后诊断工具转变为控制环路原语，支持对辐射诱导事件进行原位自适应响应，应用于量子计算错误缓解和超导量子比特粒子检测。

Abstract: Ionizing radiation from cosmic rays and gammas can induce discontinuous jumps in the environmental charge of superconducting qubits (charge jumps), causing correlated errors that challenge fault-tolerant quantum computing while simultaneously providing a detection signature for quantum sensing applications. Current detection methods operate offline, introducing latency incompatible with in-the-loop qubit control. In this paper, an online detector of charge jumps for superconducting qubits, based on a dilated causal convolutional neural network (DCCNN) designed for in-the-loop deployment on the Quantum Instrumentation Control Kit (QICK) platform, is presented. The network is trained on synthetic Ramsey tomography scans generated from qubit templates measured at the Northwestern Experimental Underground Site (NEXUS) at Fermilab, and translated to FPGA firmware via hls4ml with ap_fixed$\langle 16,6 \rangle$ quantization, reaching a per-inference latency of $6.19 μ$s on the Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216. At this operating point the DCCNN matches the detection efficiency of the established offline $χ^2$ algorithm ($0.843 \pm 0.022$ vs. $0.866 \pm 0.020$ on $|Δq| \in [0.1, 0.5] e$ at matched false-positive rate), while requiring no per-qubit hyperparameter tuning. This shifts charge-jump detection from a post-hoc diagnostic to a control-loop primitive, enabling adaptive protocols that respond to radiation-induced events in situ, with applications to quantum-computing error mitigation and to the use of superconducting qubits as particle detectors.

</details>


### [123] [On the origin of finite entanglement scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15124)
*Luke Hodgkiss,Laurens Lootens,Atsushi Ueda,Bram Vanhecke,Frank Verstraete*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过开发隐式稀疏线性求解器，解决了矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时实际扰动的开放问题，发现这些扰动与共形场论预测存在显著差异。该算法对投影纠缠对态的变分优化具有独立价值，可规避传统自动微分方法的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限纠缠标度是张量网络的基础支柱之一，但矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时诱导的实际扰动性质是一个长期未解决的开放问题。该问题对于理解张量网络方法在临界系统中的行为至关重要。

Method: 开发了一种稀疏线性求解器，以隐式方式计算二维张量网络相对于其定义参数的前向和反向导数。该算法避免了传统自动微分方法在处理投影纠缠对态变分优化时的不稳定性问题。

Result: 发现矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时诱导的实际扰动与共形场论预测的扰动存在显著差异，解决了该领域的开放问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的隐式稀疏线性求解器不仅揭示了临界系统中张量网络近似的真实扰动行为，还为投影纠缠对态的变分优化提供了一种数值稳定的新工具。

Abstract: The concept of finite entanglement scaling forms one of the pillars on which the tensor network ecosystem is built. In this paper, we resolve the open problem of determining the actual perturbations induced by matrix product state approximations of critical systems, and we demonstrate that these can be quite different than the ones predicted by conformal field theory. To that aim, we develop a sparse linear solver to calculate the forward and backward derivatives of 2-dimensional tensor networks with respect to their defining parameters in an implicit way. This algorithm is of independent interest as it provides a primitive for the variational optimization of projected entangled pair states that circumvents the instabilities plaguing traditional automatic differentiation methods.

</details>


### [124] [Periodic orbits and quantum many-body scars in integrable spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15132)
*Elena Petrova,Maksym Serbyn,Marko Ljubotina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可积XYZ/XXZ自旋1/2链中应用周期轨道框架，通过Bethe ansatz量子数识别出疤痕轨道族，揭示了零动量马格农对解耦产生等距塔结构的微观机制，并证明该结构在破坏可积性后仍持续存在，建立了周期轨道量子疤痕与可积模型代数结构之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕提供了热化动力学的例外，但其与可积性之间的关系仍不清晰，需要建立两者之间的直接联系。

Method: 将周期轨道框架应用于可积XYZ/XXZ自旋1/2链，发展能量分辨方法以定位和追踪跨谱周期轨道；通过Bethe ansatz量子数识别疤痕轨道及其支撑本征态，揭示共同弦核由零动量马格农对修饰的结构。

Result: 成功在Bethe ansatz框架内重构了疤痕本征态及其塔结构，从零动量马格农对在Bethe方程中的解耦解析推导出等距塔间距；并证明该结构在引入横向场破坏可积性后仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 建立了周期轨道量子疤痕图景与可积模型代数结构之间的直接联系，表明疤痕动力学的关键特征可在Bethe ansatz框架内解析理解。

Abstract: Quantum many-body scars provide an exception to generic thermalising dynamics, but their relation to integrability remains unclear. Here we apply the periodic-orbit framework to the integrable XYZ/XXZ spin-1/2 chain, developing an energy-resolved approach that enables us to locate and track periodic orbits across the spectrum. We identify families of scarred orbits and resolve their supporting eigenstates in terms of Bethe-ansatz quantum numbers, revealing a common string core dressed by zero-momentum magnon pairs. This allows us to reconstruct both the scarred eigenstates and the associated towers directly within the Bethe ansatz, and explain the equidistant tower spacing analytically from the decoupling of zero-momentum magnon pairs in the Bethe equations, providing a microscopic realisation of scar phenomenology in an integrable setting. We further show that this structure persists upon breaking integrability with a transverse field, with the periodic orbits continuing to govern the dynamics. Our results establish a direct connection between the periodic-orbit picture of quantum scarring and the algebraic structure of integrable models, showing that key features of scar dynamics can be understood analytically within the Bethe ansatz framework.

</details>


### [125] [Emergence and Recovery of (logical) Kochen-Specker Contextuality via Hamilton Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14629)
*Jayashree Karmakar,Biswadeep Chatterjee,Rafiuddin Gazi,Anushko Chattopadhyay,Ananya Chakraborty,Snehasish Roy Chowdhury,Amit Mukherjee,Manik Banik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Hamilton扩展，一种将KS可着色向量集转化为KS不可着色集的构造方法，揭示了逻辑KS语境性可以从非语境性向量集中涌现，并证明了六维向量是生成KS矛盾的最小父集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Kochen-Specker语境性是特定测量配置的内在属性。本文试图表明，语境性可以通过一种称为Hamilton扩展的构造性程序，从KS可着色的向量集中涌现出来，从而提供一种理解语境性起源的新视角。

Method: 针对四维实向量集定义Hamilton扩展操作：将每个实向量与一个测量语境相关联，同时在不同父向量的Hamilton扩展子代之间诱导额外的测量语境。这些涌现语境改变了兼容性结构，将KS可着色配置转化为KS不可着色配置，恢复在顶点-顶点增广下丢失的逻辑语境性。

Result: 建立了精确且最优的阈值：任意五个向量的父集的Hamilton扩展仍保持KS可着色，而适当选择的六个向量的父集已能生成逻辑KS矛盾。因此，六个向量是通过该机制生成KS矛盾的最小父集。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了语境性的一种新的结构路径，为构建紧凑型KS集提供了系统框架，对基于语境性的量子信息协议和量子非经典性的图论方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: Logical Kochen-Specker (KS) contextuality is widely regarded as an intrinsic property of specially constructed measurement configurations. We show instead that it can emerge from KS-colorable vector sets through a constructive procedure we call the Hamilton extension. Defined for four-dimensional vector sets, the Hamilton extension associates each real vector with a measurement context while inducing additional measurement contexts among Hamilton-extended children of distinct parent vectors. These emergent contexts fundamentally alter the compatibility structure, transforming KS-colorable configurations into KS-uncolorable ones and recovering logical contextuality lost under apex-vertex augmentation. We establish a sharp and optimal threshold -- the Hamilton extension of every five-vector parent set remains KS-colorable, whereas suitably chosen six-vector parent sets already generate logical KS contradictions. Thus, six vectors constitute the smallest parent set capable of generating KS contradiction through this mechanism. Our results reveal a new structural route to contextuality, provide a systematic framework for constructing compact KS sets, and have implications for contextuality-based quantum information protocols and graph-theoretic approaches to nonclassicality.

</details>


### [126] [An end-to-end quantum algorithm for weakly nonlinear plasma physics with superquadratic speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14308)
*Bjorn K. Berntson,David Jennings,Matteo Lostaglio,Scott Parker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端量子算法，用于弱非线性动理学等离子体模拟。通过Lyapunov变换下的Carleman线性嵌入、分层块编码协议和非线性信息提取子程序，克服了量子模拟中的非线性嵌入、稠密数据加载和信息提取三大瓶颈。相较于经典傅里叶-厄米谱方法，实现了指数级内存节省和超二次时间加速，并提供了严格的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动理学等离子体模拟是经典计算中的高维难题，而量子算法面临三个瓶颈：将非线性动力学嵌入线性计算、加载稠密的场-粒子相互作用数据、高效提取信息。本文旨在开发一种端到端量子算法来解决这些挑战，并为受控非线性等离子体模拟建立量子基准。

Method: 1) 利用等离子体自由能识别Lyapunov变换，在该变换下Carleman线性嵌入在认证的弱非线性区域内按截断阶数指数收敛；2) 开发分层块编码协议，利用场的空间衰减避免稀疏存取编码的多项式开销；3) 引入信息提取子程序，利用完整Carleman历史态中编码的非线性分量来改进线性可观测量估计。

Result: 该量子算法估计时空平均动能所需的门复杂度为Õ(N_F N_H^{1/2} polylog(T/ε)⋅1/ε)，量子比特数为Õ(log(N_F N_H^{1/2}T)log(1/ε))。相对于经典傅里叶-厄米谱方法，实现了指数级内存节省和超二次时间加速。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了受控非线性等离子体模拟的量子基准，证明了在弱非线性条件下量子算法可以有效地模拟动理学等离子体系统，为更复杂的等离子体量子模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear kinetic plasma simulation is high-dimensional and classically demanding, while quantum algorithms face different bottlenecks: embedding nonlinear dynamics into a linear computation, loading dense field-interaction data, and efficiently extracting information. We present an end-to-end quantum algorithm, with rigorous convergence guarantees, for a weakly nonlinear kinetic plasma model. The system describes a 3D electron-ion plasma with adiabatic electrons, kinetic ions, Debye screening, and Krook relaxation. After Fourier-Hermite truncation, the dynamics reduces to a high-dimensional quadratic ordinary differential equation. To tackle quantum bottlenecks we combine three key ingredients. First, we use a plasma free energy to identify a Lyapunov transform under which a Carleman linear embedding converges exponentially in the truncation order within a certified weakly nonlinear regime. Second, we develop a hierarchical block-encoding protocol for dense matrices, exploiting the spatial decay of the field to avoid polynomial overhead from sparse access encodings. Third, we introduce a subroutine for information extraction that exploits nonlinear components encoded in the full Carleman history state to improve the estimation of linear observables. We construct a quantum algorithm to estimate the spacetime-averaged kinetic energy using $\widetilde{O}\!\left( N_F N_H^{1/2} \operatorname{polylog}\!\left(\frac{T}ε\right)\frac{1}ε\right)$ gates and $\widetilde{O}\!\left(\log\!\left(N_F N_H^{1/2}T\right)\log\!\left(\frac{1}ε\right)\right)$ qubits, where $N_F$ and $N_H$ are the Fourier and Hermite cutoffs. Relative to a Fourier-Hermite spectral solver, this yields exponential memory savings and superquadratic improvements in time. Together, these results establish a controlled nonlinear plasma benchmark for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [127] [Backpropagating Pauli Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15184)
*Sheng-Hsuan Lin,Etienne Granet,Kevin Hémery,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泡利传播模拟的量子电路反向传播算法，用于高效计算参数梯度。该方法在计算复杂度上与标准稀疏泡利模拟技术相当，梯度精度与可观测量期望值同阶，并通过利用量子电路可逆性将内存成本降低O(n_param)倍，同时比有限差分法在函数评估上高效O(n_param)倍。实验展示了该方法在伊辛模型和海森堡模型低能态制备以及时间演化电路压缩中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子电路的经典优化中，高效且准确地计算参数梯度是关键挑战。现有方法如反向模式自动微分内存成本高，有限差分法需要大量函数评估，因此需要一种兼顾计算效率、内存成本和梯度精度的新方法。

Method: 利用泡利传播模拟开发反向传播算法，通过量子电路可逆性减少内存开销，实现梯度计算的计算复杂度与标准稀疏泡利模拟相当，同时保证梯度精度与可观测量期望值同阶。

Result: 该方法在横向场伊辛模型（一维、二维、三维）和三维海森堡模型的低能态制备电路优化，以及二维时间演化电路压缩任务中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该算法能够实现高效准确的量子电路经典优化，支持在优化过程中监测和正则化算子复杂度的度量（如算子稳定子Rényi熵），适用于态制备和时间演化压缩等应用。

Abstract: We develop a backpropagation algorithm for evaluating parameter gradients in quantum circuits using Pauli propagation simulation. The method has computational complexity comparable to that of standard sparse Pauli simulation techniques, while producing gradients whose accuracy is of the same order as the corresponding observable expectation values. By exploiting the reversibility of quantum circuits, the algorithm reduces the memory cost by a factor of $\mathcal{O}(n_\text{param})$ compared with conventional reverse-mode automatic differentiation, where $n_\text{param}$ denotes the number of parameters in the circuit. Compared with finite difference methods, the algorithm is $\mathcal{O}(n_\text{param})$ more efficient in function evaluations. These features enable efficient and accurate classical optimization of quantum circuits for applications such as state preparation and time-evolution compression, while also allowing operator-complexity measures such as the operator stabilizer Rényi entropy to be monitored and regularized during optimization. We demonstrate the method by optimizing low-energy state-preparation circuits for transverse-field Ising models in one, two, and three dimensions and for the three-dimensional Heisenberg model, and by compressing two-dimensional time-evolution circuits.

</details>


### [128] [General theory of monitored Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14311)
*Oriol Morguí-Sancho,Gian Luca Giorgi,Gonzalo Manzano,Roberta Zambrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于间接量子测量的监控量子储备池计算（QRC）统一理论框架，将投影、弱、部分和耗散监测协议整合在一个操作框架内，证明测量反作用作为可控资源可为QRC提供有效耗散和非幺正动力学，推导了监控动力学满足回波状态、衰减记忆和输入可分性的普适条件，揭示不同监控方案是计算能力的定性不同路径，而非可互换的参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRC研究将不同监控方案孤立处理，缺乏统一物理原理来理解测量在在线量子储备池中的作用，特别是测量反作用从干扰源到计算资源的角色转变缺乏系统性理论。

Method: 发展基于间接量子测量的通用理论，统一投影测量、弱测量、部分测量和耗散监测协议；推导监控动力学满足回波状态性质、衰减记忆和输入可分性的充分必要条件；通过共同参考动力学比较不同监控方案。

Result: 测量反作用可作为可控资源，为QRC提供必要的有效耗散和非幺正动力学；给出了监控动力学满足严格收缩性的充要条件；证明不同监控协议是计算能力的定性不同路径，而非可互换的参数化方案；时间复用可进一步塑造信息提取与测量扰动之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 建立了在线监控量子储备池计算的统一理论基础，将量子测量工程确立为跨量子平台设计储备池架构的系统方法。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) provides a powerful framework for processing temporal data using quantum dynamics, but incorporating measurements into the reservoir remains a fundamental challenge and distinctive feature with respect to classical settings. The induced back-action can vary from a source of disturbance to a computational resource, as measurement deeply modifies the dynamics underlying temporal processing. Existing approaches have treated specific monitoring schemes independently, missing the common physical principles governing online quantum reservoirs. Here we develop a general theory of monitored quantum reservoir computing based on indirect quantum measurements, which unifies projective, weak, partial, and dissipative monitoring protocols within a single operational framework. Measurement back-action can serve as a controllable resource, providing the effective dissipation and non-unital dynamics required for successful QRC, even when the underlying unmonitored evolution is unsuitable. We derive general criteria under which monitored dynamics satisfy the echo-state property, fading memory, and input separability, including a necessary and sufficient condition for emergent strict contractivity. By comparing different monitoring schemes under a common reference dynamics, we show that these protocols are not interchangeable parameterizations to be optimized for peak performance, but rather constitute qualitatively distinct routes to computational capability, each enabled by the interplay between information extraction and measurement-induced disturbance -- a trade-off that can be further shaped through time multiplexing. Our results provide a unified theoretical foundation for online monitored quantum reservoir computing and establish quantum measurement engineering as a systematic approach for designing reservoir architectures across different quantum platforms.

</details>


### [129] [Towards a monolithic platform for coupling superconducting circuits to low-loss microwave phonons in AlScN on 4H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14319)
*Yuanchen Deng,William W. Roberts,Sueli Skinner-Ramos,Dalton Anderson,Katherine Hewey,Xingyu Du,Michael Miller,Brandon Smith,Hwijong Lee,Pingping Chen,Charles Thomas Harris,Roy H. Olsson,Lisa Hackett,Rupert Lewis,Matt Eichenfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并表征了一种单片式量子声学平台，将铝基超导电路与压电AlScN-on-SiC集成在同一SiC衬底上，无需悬空结构，实现了超导量子器件的兼容性和低损耗声子传输，为混合超导-声子量子系统提供了可行平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导-声子量子处理在腔QED、测量基量子计算等领域具有前景，但需要一种可扩展的平台，既能利用声子的紧凑尺寸、低损耗和强耦合优势，又不降低超导电路性能，且无需悬空声子结构。

Method: 采用选择性去除AlScN的方法，在SiC衬底上分别保留AlScN-on-SiC区域用于声子转换，并在裸露SiC上直接制备铝超导微波谐振器，形成单片集成架构。

Result: Al-on-SiC谐振器相干寿命达2.9 μs，与铝超导量子器件兼容；AlScN-on-SiC低温表面声波延迟线在4.05 GHz下声子传播损耗低，对应声子寿命约7.6 μs；此前展示的机电耦合系数约4.3%，理论上限8%。

Conclusion: Al-on-SiC/AlScN-on-SiC是一种有前景的单片平台，可用于集成超导微波电路与压电声子组件，支持量子声学网络和混合量子系统。

Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-phonon quantum processing is promising for cavity QED, measurement-based quantum computing, and other quantum applications. Relative to microwave photons at the same frequency, phonons can provide ultra-compact footprints, extremely low losses, and greater connectivity. Phonons can also couple strongly to superconducting circuits through the piezoelectric effect. However, this promise rests on scalable platforms that achieve these benefits without degrading superconducting circuit performance. This motivates a monolithic platform combining low phononic loss, strong electromechanical coupling, and superconducting-circuit compatibility without requiring suspended phononics. Here, we characterize a monolithic quantum acoustic platform combining aluminum superconducting circuits on exposed silicon carbide (SiC) with piezoelectric aluminum scandium nitride (AlScN) on SiC for integrated phononics. This architecture is enabled by selective removal of AlScN from selected chip regions, allowing aluminum superconducting microwave resonators to be fabricated directly on the SiC while preserving adjacent AlScN-on-SiC regions for phonon transduction. The resulting Al-on-SiC resonators exhibit a coherent lifetime of 2.9 μs, demonstrating compatibility with aluminum superconducting quantum devices. In parallel, cryogenic surface acoustic delay-line measurements on the retained AlScN-on-SiC regions show low phononic propagation loss at 4.05 GHz, corresponding to an estimated phonon lifetime of 7.6 μs. Together with a previously demonstrated electromechanical coupling coefficient of about 4.3% and a theoretical upper bound of 8%, these results establish Al-on-SiC/AlScN-on-SiC as a promising monolithic platform for integrating superconducting microwave circuits with piezoelectric phononic components for quantum acoustic networking and hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [130] [Robustness of periodicity in Grover walks under a magnetic vector potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14797)
*Hiroto Sekido,Etsuo Segawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁矢势对有限图上周期性情食者游走的影响，发现其对周期性的鲁棒性依赖于图的谱结构，特别地，当图存在非简单特征值时，导出了一个刻画鲁棒性的Hermitian矩阵，并证明扰动动力学渐近地由该矩阵生成的连续时间量子游走描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁矢势作为微扰对周期性情食者游走周期性的影响，理解其鲁棒性及其背后的谱机制。

Method: 通过量子图框架引入磁矢势，将情食者游走作为特例；分析图的谱结构，导出非简单特征值情形下的Hermitian矩阵，并建立渐近描述。

Result: 发现扰动对周期性的影响取决于图的谱结构；当图存在非简单特征值时，存在一个Hermitian矩阵刻画鲁棒性；扰动后的动力学渐近等价于该Hermitian矩阵生成的连续时间量子游走。

Conclusion: 磁矢势扰动下周期性情食者游走的渐近行为可由连续时间量子游走描述，其生成元为依赖于图谱结构的Hermitian矩阵。

Abstract: We study the effect of magnetic vector potentials on periodic Grover walks on finite graphs. The magnetic vector potential is introduced through the framework of quantum graphs, which induces the Grover walk as a special case. We regard the vector potential as a perturbation of a periodic Grover walk and investigate the robustness of its periodicity. Our analysis reveals that the response to such perturbations depends on the spectral structure of the underlying graph. In particular, when the graph possesses at least one non-simple eigenvalue, we derive a Hermitian matrix that characterizes the robustness of its periodicity. As a consequence, we show that the perturbed dynamics is asymptotically described by a continuous-time quantum walk generated by this Hermitian matrix.

</details>


### [131] [Collapse and Inversion of the Josephson Potential in a Strongly Driven Superconducting Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14344)
*Sercan Deve,Marius Villiers,Marijn A. Morssink,Robin C. Dekker,Gary A. Steele*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过在超导电路中设计无有害跃迁的transmon-谐振器系统，发现了强驱动下约瑟夫森势阱的坍缩与反转现象，并实验证实了transmon在其不稳定平衡点处的动态稳定化（类似于倒立摆），揭示了强驱动超导电路的新限制并为量子态控制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中增加微波驱动功率虽能提高量子态操控效率，但会触发到非受控态的有害跃迁。虽然通过电路对称性和参数设计可缓解此问题，但杂散电路模式会破坏高功率下的鲁棒性。本文旨在工程化一个无有害跃迁的系统，并探索强驱动下的新物理现象。

Method: 设计了一个无有害跃迁的transmon-谐振器系统，通过施加高功率微波驱动，利用谱学和读出实验观测约瑟夫森势的坍缩与反转现象。

Result: 实验证实了驱动势能随功率增加先归零后反转，实现了transmon在其不稳定平衡点处的动态稳定化，该现象类似于倒立摆的稳定。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了强驱动超导电路除跃迁诱导效应之外的新限制，同时约瑟夫森势的动态重整化为超导电路控制和噪声鲁棒量子态的自主稳定提供了新途径。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits embedding Josephson junctions leverage microwave drives for control and measurement of quantum states. Although increasing the drive power is desirable for improving the efficiency of these operations, it eventually triggers unwanted transitions to uncontrolled states. While careful choice of circuit symmetries and parameters can mitigate these effects, the presence of spurious circuit modes spoils the resilience to high power. In this work, we engineer a transmon-resonator system free of any detrimental unwanted transitions. This resilience enables us to access drive powers at which we uncover a remarkable physical phenomenon: the collapse and inversion of the Josephson potential. Through spectroscopy and readout experiments, we confirm that the driven potential goes to zero and inverts as the power increases. The inversion corresponds to the dynamical stabilization of the transmon at its unstable equilibrium point, directly analogous to an inverted pendulum. This result reveals a new limitation of strongly driven superconducting circuits beyond drive-induced transitions. In addition, the dynamical renormalization of the Josephson potential opens up novel avenues for the control of superconducting circuits, and the autonomous stabilization of noise-resilient quantum states.

</details>


### [132] [Coulomb blockade in microscopic material defects as a source of decoherence and noise in solid-state quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15252)
*R. Banerjee,L. P. Lindoy,M. Hegedus,A. Hutcheson,T. Hawkins,E. Daghigh-Ahmadi,S. Samaddar,T. Barker,J. P. Goff,A. Ya. Tzalenchuk,I. Rungger,S. E. de Graaf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现超导电路中普遍存在由金属颗粒中的库仑阻塞和微波驱动电荷隧穿引起的退相干机制，该机制与传统二能级系统缺陷同样常见且有害，挑战了现有认知，并提出了通过消除金属颗粒来改善量子器件相干性的实际路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子器件的退相干机制与其微观材料起源之间的关联尚不明确，这阻碍了有效的缓解策略开发，限制了相干时间的提升。

Method: 使用扫描栅极显微镜对工作中的超导电路进行实时成像，表征不同器件中的多个缺陷，并与传统表征技术进行对比。

Result: 发现金属颗粒引起的库仑阻塞和微波驱动电荷隧穿是一种新的退相干机制；这种缺陷与传统二能级系统缺陷同样普遍，但物理机制根本不同；常规表征技术会将其误归因于其他过程。

Conclusion: 金属颗粒缺陷挑战了"相干寿命主要受限于二能级系统缺陷"的主流范式；在制造过程中消除金属颗粒是抑制该机制、改善超导量子器件相干性和降低噪声的可行途径。

Abstract: A critical limitation of solid-state quantum devices arises from the materials from which they are fabricated: uncontrolled surfaces, interfaces, and structural imperfections introduce numerous sources of loss and decoherence. Despite extensive efforts, linking these decoherence mechanisms to their microscopic material origins -- essential for developing effective mitigation strategies -- remains an outstanding challenge that has slowed coherence improvements. Here, using scanning gate microscopy on live superconducting circuits we identify a previously unrecognised decoherence mechanism originating from Coulomb blockade and microwave-driven charge tunnelling in metallic grains. Such grains are ubiquitous in thin-film devices fabricated by standard lithography processes. By characterising multiple defects across different devices, we find such defects to be as common and as debilitating to device performance as two-level system (TLS) defects, while originating from a fundamentally different physical mechanism. Importantly, conventional characterisation techniques would misattribute this loss to other, microwave power-independent processes. Our observations thus reveal a widespread source of decoherence in superconducting circuits, challenging the prevailing paradigm that coherence lifetimes are primarily limited by TLS defects. Eliminating metallic grains during fabrication provides a clear and practical route to suppress this mechanism, offering a pathway towards improved coherence and reduced noise in microwave-based solid-state quantum devices.

</details>


### [133] [Unified monogamy and polygamy relations for multipartite systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14370)
*Yue Cao,Naihuan Jing,Kailash Misra,Yiling Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对满足γ次幂单配性不等式和δ次幂多配性不等式的纠缠度量，引入了一个基于参数m≥1的统一可调框架，推导出两族层次化精炼不等式：适用于α≥mγ的收紧α次幂单配性关系，以及适用于(m-1)δ<β≤mδ的收紧β次幂多配性关系。随着m增大，界限逐步收紧，最优单配性界呈现为α的分段函数。通过并发性和辅助并发性分析验证了该方法的普适性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠单配性和多配性不等式在刻画多体纠缠分布时存在局限性，缺乏统一的、可调的精炼框架来提供更紧致的界限，从而更精确地表征纠缠资源在多体系统中的分配特性。

Method: 引入参数化框架m≥1，基于γ次幂单配性和δ次幂多配性不等式，推导出两族层次化不等式：α次幂单配性关系（α≥mγ）和β次幂多配性关系（(m-1)δ<β≤mδ）。通过并发性和辅助并发性进行解析比较和数值评估。

Result: 新不等式随m增大逐步收紧，最优单配性界呈现为α的分段函数，在α跨越整数阈值时激活额外修正项；推广并加强了现有单配性和多配性关系。

Conclusion: 该层次化参数化方法为量子通信、量子网络和多体量子信息处理提供了增强且灵活的工具，能够更清晰地刻画纠缠分布。

Abstract: For a bipartite entanglement measure $\mathcal{E}$ that satisfies the $γ$th-power monogamy inequality (Eq.~\eqref{e:chap1-ineq1}), and for its assisted counterpart $\mathcal{E}_a$ that obeys the $δ$th-power polygamy inequality (Eq.~\eqref{e:chap1-ineq2}), we introduce a unified, tunable framework indexed by a parameter $m\geq1$. Within this framework, we derive two hierarchical families of refined inequalities: 
  a tightened $α$-power monogamy relation for $\mathcal{E}$, valid for all $α\geq mγ$; 
  a tightened $β$-power polygamy relation for $\mathcal{E}_a$, applicable for $(m-1)δ< β\leq mδ$. 
  As $m$ increases, the bounds become progressively tighter, recovering known results at $m=1$. Notably, the optimal monogamy bound emerges as a piecewise function of $α$, with additional correction terms activated as $α$ crosses successive integer thresholds, thereby offering a sharper characterization of entanglement distribution.
  We demonstrate that our results generalize and strengthen existing monogamy and polygamy relations through analytical comparisons and numerical evaluations using concurrence and concurrence of assistance. This hierarchical, parameterized approach offers enhanced and flexible tools for applications in quantum communication, quantum networks, and multipartite quantum information processing.

</details>


### [134] [Locality of deep thermalisation through the lens of entanglement teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15276)
*Saptarshi Mandal,Alan Sherry,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究因对互补系统进行投影测量而在子系统上出现的通用量子态系综的深层热化现象。当子系统分为两个在幺正动力学下始终因果断开的子区域时，深层热化的出现根本上受限于测量诱导的纠缠传送。尽管环境测量在断开分区之间产生纠缠（看似非局域），但一般局域相互作用系统表现出涌现的局域性：深层热化和纠缠传送的时间尺度与子区域间距成对数关系。只有在特殊电路中，测量结果的随机性被完美传递到子系统态系综时，深层热化时间尺度才为有限值，导致真正的非局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深层热化现象中的局域性（或非局域性）本质，特别是当子系统被分为两个始终因果断开的子区域时，测量如何影响量子态系综的涌现。

Method: 将子系统分为两个因果断开的子区域，分析投影测量在环境上对这两个子区域间纠缠传送的影响，研究一般局域相互作用系统与特殊电路两种情况下的深层热化时间尺度。

Result: 一般局域相互作用系统中，深层热化和纠缠传送的时间尺度与子区域间距成对数关系（涌现的局域性）；特殊电路中，测量结果随机性被完美传递时，深层热化时间尺度为有限值（真正非局域性）。

Conclusion: 深层热化在因果断开子区域间的出现受限于测量诱导的纠缠传送，其局域性特征取决于系统类型：一般局域系统表现出涌现的局域性，而特殊电路可产生真正的非局域性。

Abstract: Deep thermalisation characterises the emergence of universal quantum state ensembles on subsystems due to projective measurements on their complement. We study the notion of locality, or lack thereof, in this phenomenon by considering a subsystem partitioned into two disjoint subregions which remain causally disconnected at all times under unitary dynamics. We show that the onset of deep thermalisation in this geometry is fundamentally bounded by measurement-induced entanglement teleportation between the subregions. While measurements on the environment generate entanglement across the disconnected partitions -- suggesting an apparent non-locality -- we demonstrate that generic locally interacting systems exhibit an emergent locality. Specifically, the timescales for both deep thermalisation and entanglement teleportation scale logarithmically with the distance separating the subregions. Exceptions to this include special circuits where the randomness of the measurement outcomes is perfectly transmitted to the ensemble of states of the subsystem, conditioned on the outcomes; in such cases the timescale for deep thermalisation is finite leading to genuine non-locality.

</details>


### [135] [State-independent uncertainty relations on multipartite spin $1/2$-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14376)
*Yiling Wang,Kailash Misra,Naihuan Jing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用表示论框架（Clebsch-Gordan分解和总自旋方差极值分析）推导出从一到五体自旋1/2系统的精确方差基态无关不确定关系（SIURs），发现奇偶性结构二分性：奇数n系统具有严格正通用下界，偶数n系统在平凡扇区方差可为零但约化空间保持正下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的方差基态无关不确定关系（SIURs）在二分体以上系统中长期缺乏解析解，现有工作仅对单体和二分体系统有精确结果，而多体系统的解析解一直未解决。

Method: 采用表示论框架，利用Clebsch-Gordan分解和总自旋方差的极值分析，推导集体自旋1/2系统中多体SIURs的精确界。

Result: 推导出最多到五体系统的精确态无关下界，揭示了奇偶性结构二分性：奇数n（如n=3时Δ²≥4/11）有严格正界，偶数n（如n=4时Δ²≥1/8）在平凡扇区方差为零但约化空间保持正下界。

Conclusion: 建立了首个统一的、基于代数的多体方差基SIURs框架，填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations quantify fundamental limits on simultaneous measurement of quantum observables. While conventional formulations are state-dependent, state-independent uncertainty relations (SIURs) impose universal bounds determined solely by the algebraic structure of the operators, with applications across metrology, quantum cryptography, and entanglement detection. Despite extensive study, exact analytical variance-based SIURs have so far been established primarily for one- and bi-partite systems, while entropic SIURs have been extended to multipartite and memory-assisted settings through information-theoretic constructions. In contrast, exact variance-based SIURs beyond the bipartite level have remained analytically unresolved.} Here we develop a representation-theoretic framework for multipartite SIURs in collective spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ systems. Using the Clebsch--Gordan decomposition and extremal analysis of total spin variance, we derive exact state-independent bounds up to quintipartite systems. A clear structural dichotomy emerges: odd $n$ systems exhibit strictly positive universal bounds (e.g., $Δ^2(\mathfrak{su}_2)\!\ge\!4/11$ for $n=3$), whereas even $n$ admit vanishing variance on trivial sectors but retain positive reduced-space bounds (e.g., $Δ^2(\mathfrak{su}_2)\!\ge\!1/8$ for $n=4$). These results establish the first unified, algebraic framework for multipartite variance-based SIURs in qubit ensembles.

</details>


### [136] [Superadditivity for Entanglement-Assisted Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15151)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Mario Berta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 虽然纠缠辅助信道容量是可加的（即联合编码不能提高最终通信速率），但本文证明Petz-Rényi信道信息在α∈[1/2,1)时严格超可加，表明纠缠辅助随机编码的可靠性（错误指数）可以通过多副本使用得到真正增强，且这一现象在测量信道（纠缠破坏信道）中已被解析证明，由可分经典相关的两副本信道输入边缘分布即可实现，无需传输系统间的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"可加性意味着联合编码无益"这一直观认知，探究纠缠辅助通信场景下信道容量的可加性是否也意味着可靠性（错误指数）的可加性。

Method: 通过数学证明Petz-Rényi信道信息在α∈[1/2,1)范围内的严格超可加性，并以测量信道（纠缠破坏信道）为具体实例进行解析分析。

Result: Petz-Rényi信道信息严格超可加，即使纠缠辅助容量保持可加；该现象在测量信道中成立，且可由可分经典相关的两副本输入实现，无需纠缠。

Conclusion: 信道使用间的关联虽不能提高纠缠辅助通信的最终速率，但可以增强其通信可靠性。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted capacity of a quantum channel admits an additive single-letter characterization, implying that joint encodings across channel uses cannot increase the ultimate communication rate. Here, we show that this additive picture does not extend to communication reliability. Specifically, we prove that the Petz-Rényi channel information can be strictly superadditive for every $α\in[1/2,1)$, yielding a genuine multi-copy enhancement of the entanglement-assisted random-coding error exponent, even though the entanglement-assisted capacity remains additive. We establish this phenomenon analytically already for measurement channels, which are entanglement-breaking and have additive unassisted capacity. Remarkably, this strict superadditivity is witnessed by a separable, classically correlated two-copy channel-input marginal, demonstrating that no entanglement between the transmitted systems is required. Our results show that, although correlations across channel uses cannot increase the ultimate rate of entanglement-assisted communication, they can enhance its reliability.

</details>


### [137] [Imaginarity as a Resource within Quantum Coherence: Geometric Decomposition and Operational Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14435)
*Meng-Li Guo,Nannan Guan,Bo Li,Bin Hu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个严格的几何分解框架，将量子相干性中的虚部（imaginarity）识别为一种基本资源，揭示了相干性、虚部与剩余资源之间的普遍层次关系，并展示了虚部与可用相干性之间的可转换性以及在对角哈密顿量下的互补振荡动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子相干性理论未能充分辨识虚部（imaginarity）作为独立资源的作用，缺乏对其与其他相干组分之间关系的严格刻画。

Method: 采用几何分解方法将相干性划分为虚部成分与残余成分；针对双体系统推导非局域关联与虚部对局域相干性生成的理论界限；设计显式操作协议实现虚部到可用相干性的转换；分析对角哈密顿量下的动力学演化特性。

Result: 建立了相干性各组分间的普遍层次关系；给出了双体系统中非局域关联与虚部约束局域相干性的显式界限；证明了虚部与可用相干性在物理允许操作下的直接可转换性；揭示了对角哈密顿量下总相干性守恒而虚部与残余相干性互补振荡的动力学规律。

Conclusion: 虚部是量子相干性的基本构成资源，所建立的几何框架具有完全一般性，为分布式量子技术中的资源管理提供了具体洞察。

Abstract: We establish a rigorous framework that identifies imaginarity as a fundamental resource inherent in quantum coherence. By means of a geometric decomposition, we partition coherence into distinct imaginarity and residual components, thereby revealing a universal hierarchical relationship among these resources. For bipartite systems, this decomposition provides explicit bounds on the extent to which nonlocal correlations and imaginarity limit local coherence generation. Furthermore, we devise an explicit operational protocol that converts imaginarity into usable coherence, demonstrating the direct interconvertibility of these resources under physically admissible operations. The dynamical evolution under diagonal Hamiltonians is fully characterized, showing that while total coherence is conserved, imaginarity and residual coherence exhibit complementary oscillations. Our results provide a rigorous geometric and operational characterization of imaginarity as a fundamental constituent of quantum coherence, offering concrete insights for resource management in distributed quantum technologies. The geometric framework and theoretical bounds established herein are fully general, while the explicit conversion protocol and dynamical analysis serve as a compelling proof-of-principle demonstration in qubit systems.

</details>


### [138] [Counterexamples to additivity of minimum output $p$-Rényi entropy of quantum channels for $p>3/4$ and $0\leq p<1/4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15210)
*Debbie Leung,Benjamin Lovitz,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意Rényi阶数p>3/4或0≤p<1/4，存在有限维投影诱导量子信道使得最小输出p-Rényi熵的可加性失效，将未解区间缩小至[1/4,3/4]，并改进了von Neumann熵可加性违背的输出维度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出熵的可加性是量子信息论的核心问题，此前仅对p>1、p=1及p接近0时已知非可加性，而0<p<1的大部分区间尚未解决。

Method: 结合两种相关随机投影构造：对p>3/4使用乘积共轭Bell态见证，对p<1/4使用转置补秩缺陷见证。

Result: 证明了对于p>3/4或0≤p<1/4，存在有限维投影诱导量子信道使得最小输出p-Rényi熵的可加性失效，并将未解区间压缩至[1/4,3/4]。

Conclusion: 进一步缩小了Rényi熵可加性问题的开放区间，同时改进了von Neumann熵可加性违背的输出维度下界。

Abstract: Additivity of minimum output entropies is a central problem in quantum information theory. Nonadditivity is known for every Rényi order $p>1$, at the von Neumann point $p=1$, and near $p=0$, while most of the interval $0<p<1$ has remained open. We prove that for every Rényi order $p$ satisfying either $p>3/4$ or $0\leq p<1/4$, there exist finite-dimensional projection-induced quantum channels such that additivity of the minimum output $p$-Rényi entropy fails. The proof combines two correlated random-projection constructions: a product-conjugate Bell-state witness for $p>3/4$, and a transpose-complement rank-defect witness for $p<1/4$. Thus the unresolved part of $0<p<1$ is reduced to $[1/4,3/4]$. Our estimates also improve the output dimension threshold for additivity violation of minimum output von Neumann entropy, first established in Belinschi, Collins and Nechida.

</details>


### [139] [Improving Dynamical Decoupling for Trapped-Ion QCCD Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14441)
*William M. Watkins,Leigh M. Norris,Ross Hutson,Maxwell Urmey,Peter Siegfried,Charles H. Baldwin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究调度误差对量子充电耦合器件（QCCD）中动态解耦（DD）的影响，发现调度引起的定时误差会增强DD对低频噪声的敏感性，使得在捕获离子平台上将DD脉冲频率提升至2 Hz以上通常适得其反。为此，提出一种利用空闲期机会性插入重聚焦脉冲的实时DD协议，并在Quantinuum H2-1上通过Ramsey延迟实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QCCD架构中，量子比特的传输和控制会引入随机脉冲延迟，影响动态解耦的效率。现有DD策略未充分考虑这些调度误差的影响，导致在捕获离子平台上高频率DD脉冲反而降低性能。因此，需要分析调度误差对DD的影响机制，并开发更优的存储错误抑制策略。

Method: 利用滤波器函数形式化分析调度诱导定时误差下DD的性能；通过数值模拟研究捕获离子平台上典型存储错误下的DD效果；提出一种实时DD协议，在空闲期间机会性地插入重聚焦脉冲。

Result: 调度诱导的定时误差增强了标准DD对低频涨落的敏感性；数值表明捕获离子平台上DD脉冲频率超过2 Hz时因调度误差而适得其反；提出的实时DD协议在Quantinuum H2-1上通过Ramsey延迟实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 调度误差是QCCD中DD性能的关键限制因素，机会性实时DD策略能有效缓解该问题，为捕获离子量子计算平台的内存错误抑制提供了实用方案。

Abstract: We examine the impact of scheduling errors on dynamical decoupling (DD) in trapped-ion quantum charge-coupled devices (QCCDs) and develop better strategies for reducing memory errors. In the QCCD architecture, qubit transport and control introduce stochastic pulse delays that impact the efficiency of DD. Using the filter-function formalism, we analyze the performance of DD in the presence of scheduling-induced timing errors, showing that they increase the sensitivity of standard DD to low-frequency fluctuations. For typical memory errors in trapped-ion platforms, we numerically demonstrate that increasing the DD pulse frequency beyond 2 Hz is generally counterproductive due to scheduling-induced timing errors. Furthermore, we introduce a real-time DD protocol that inserts refocusing pulses opportunistically during idle periods. We demonstrate our methods on Quantinuum H2-1 with Ramsey delay-type experiments.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum correlations of tripartite mixed states in the black hole quantum atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14462)
*Anqi Zhang,Yanze Zheng,Xiaofen Huang,Tinggui Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞量子大气中三体混合态的量子态纹理、真多体纠缠和三体非局域性，发现这三个量的极值均位于近视界区域并随局域霍金温度升高向外移动，且三体非局域性比真多体纠缠更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞量子大气中局域霍金效应对三体混合态的不同量子信息特性（态纹理、真多体纠缠、三体非局域性）的影响，并揭示这些特性在黑洞视界附近的统一行为。

Method: 将Hartle-Hawking局域温度引入Bogoliubov系数，以此刻画局域霍金效应对物理可及和不可及约化态的影响，分析三体量子关联量的变化规律。

Result: 三个量的极值均位于近视界区域并随局域霍金温度升高向外移动；三体非局域性比真多体纠缠更脆弱，在更强的局域霍金效应下被抑制。

Conclusion: 研究为黑洞量子大气影响下的三体混合态提供了密度矩阵重构、纠缠重分布和非局域性的统一刻画。

Abstract: We investigate quantum state texture, genuine multipartite entanglement, and tripartite nonlocality of a tripartite mixed state in the black hole quantum atmosphere. By introducing the Hartle-Hawking local temperature into the Bogoliubov coefficients, we characterize the influence of the local Hawking effect on both physically accessible and inaccessible reduced states. We find that the extrema of these three quantities all lie in the same near horizon region and shift outward with increasing local Hawking temperature, coinciding with the peak region of the local Hawking temperature and indicating that different aspects of tripartite quantum information are most sensitive to the local Hawking effect in the same atmospheric region. In contrast to genuine multipartite entanglement, tripartite nonlocality is more fragile and is suppressed under stronger local Hawking effects. These results provide a unified characterization of density matrix restructuring, entanglement redistribution, and nonlocality in tripartite mixed states affected by the black hole quantum atmosphere.

</details>


### [141] [Perturbation Analysis of Maximal Quantum Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14469)
*Zijia Zhao,Shuixin Xiao,Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大量子泄漏（MQL）在量子编码受到扰动时的敏感性，建立了基于迹距离的连续性上界并证明其可达性，还推导了基于保真度和相对熵的充分条件来约束MQL的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大量子泄漏（MQL）虽然已有精确刻画，但其对实现不完美（即量子编码扰动）的鲁棒性尚未被系统地研究。

Method: 1. 建立基于迹距离的连续性上界；2. 通过反例证明该上界是可达到的；3. 推导基于保真度和相对熵的充分条件；4. 通过数值实验说明这些条件的松紧程度。

Result: 得到了MQL在扰动下的连续性上界（基于迹距离），并证明该界是紧的；保真度和相对熵条件虽然可作为充分条件，但数值实验表明这些条件较为宽松。

Conclusion: 最大量子泄漏对量子编码的扰动具有可量化的鲁棒性，迹距离上界是紧的，而保真度和相对熵条件在实际中可能偏保守。

Abstract: Maximal quantum leakage (MQL) is a worst-case information leakage measure that quantifies an adversary's inference advantage gained from accessing quantum encoding of classical data with arbitrary measurements. While MQL admits an exact characterization for a given ensemble of quantum states, its robustness to implementation imperfections has not been systematically studied. In this paper, we analyze the sensitivity of maximal quantum leakage under perturbations of the quantum encoding. We establish a continuity bound in terms of the trace distance between ideal and perturbed quantum states, and show, via an example, that this bound is attainable. We further derive fidelity-based and relative-entropy-based sufficient conditions for bounding the variation of maximal quantum leakage, and illustrate numerically that these conditions can be loose.

</details>


### [142] [A generalized variational quantum linear solver on photonic platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14477)
*Kang Gao,Zhao-An Wang,Ze-Guo Wang,Mao-Mao Huang,Hai Wei,Kai Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光子计算平台上实验验证了广义变分量子线性求解器（VQLS），系统求解了复域和有限域F2上的四维线性方程组。针对复域中的病态奇异问题引入Tikhonov正则化扰动项，并将VQLS扩展到有限域F2，通过重新设计代价函数引入模2运算和约束，使其天然无奇异，适用于稳定子编码理论、解码器和量子门序列设计等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQLS主要针对复域非奇异系统，但实际应用中常遇到病态或奇异问题，且缺乏在有限域上的推广。本文旨在解决这些局限性，拓展VQLS的适用范围和实用性。

Method: 在光子计算平台上，系统求解四维线性方程组；针对复域奇异系统，引入类似Tikhonov正则化的扰动项；针对有限域F2，重新设计代价函数，加入模2运算并对解向量施加约束。

Result: 成功在复域求解了非奇异和奇异（病态）系统，并在有限域F2上实现了无奇异的VQLS，验证了其在稳定子编码等场景的适用性。

Conclusion: 本工作展示了VQLS在量子计算中的实际潜力，为其实用化提供了坚实的实验基础和方法论指导。

Abstract: Based on a photonic computing platform, we experimentally validate a generalized variational quantum linear solver (VQLS) by systematically solving four-dimensional linear equation systems across different fields. In the complex field, in addition to solving non-singular systems that admit a unique solution, we investigate ill-conditioned problems arising from singularity--an issue frequently encountered in practical applications. To tackle these challenges, we introduce perturbation terms, a treatment inspired by Tikhonov regularization, and develop an algorithm capable of handling a wide range of systems. Furthermore, we extend the VQLS to the finite field F2 by redesigning the cost function to incorporate modulo 2 and imposing several constraints on the solution vector. This modulo 2 VQLS is inherently free from singularity. It is adapt to stabilizer coding theory and may find applications in areas such as decoders and the design of quantum gate sequences. Therefore, our work demonstrates the practical potential of VQLS in quantum computing, providing a solid experimental foundation and methodological guidance for its real-world applications.

</details>


### [143] [Syndrome-as-Header: A Quantum Label-Switching Architecture via Uncorrectable Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14484)
*IlKwon Sohn,Ju-Bong Kim,Kwangil Bae,Wooyeong Song,Wonhyuk Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Syndrome-as-Header (SAH)架构，通过在量子编码载荷的纠错症状结构中嵌入路由标签，实现无需外部经典头的量子标签交换，消除了控制面时序不确定性导致的对准预算失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子网络点对点扩展依赖OBS或QW等方案，其外部经典头的处理抖动必须与飞行中量子载荷保持对齐，导致保护窗口或光纤延迟线预算失败，无法容忍控制面时序不确定性。

Method: 提出SAH架构：利用不可纠正错误注入(UEI)将流标签映射为参考症状，构建症状空间报头码本；核心路由器提取症状报头、抑制可纠正信道误差分量并交换标签，无需测量或解码逻辑载荷；支持FAST转发和VERIFICATION两种模式。

Result: 在NSFNet时序基准测试中，SAH消除了外部头/载荷对准条件，时序不确定性表现为内存驻留时间和交付延迟而非对准预算损失；在足够长的内存相干时间下，高抖动场景中SAH的接受率和吞吐量均优于OBS+QEC和QW+QEC。

Conclusion: SAH通过症状级标签交换有效解决了量子网络分组转发中对准时序瓶颈，为可扩展量子网络提供了一种免外部头对齐的鲁棒路由架构。

Abstract: Scaling quantum networks beyond point-to-point links requires packet forwarding that can tolerate control-plane timing uncertainty. Existing optical-burst switching (OBS) and quantum-wrapper-based packet switching (QW) rely on external classical headers whose jitter-prone processing must remain aligned with in-flight quantum payloads, creating guard-window or fiber-delay-line budget failures. This paper proposes Syndrome-as-Header (SAH), a quantum label-switching architecture that embeds routing labels into the syndrome structure of an encoded payload. The proposed scheme uses Uncorrectable Error Injection (UEI) to map flow labels to reference syndromes and builds a syndrome-space header codebook whose decoding regions remain distinguishable under correctable channel-induced syndrome deviations. Core routers extract the syndrome header, suppress the correctable residual channel-error component, and swap labels without measuring or decoding the logical payload. SAH supports FAST forwarding and VERIFICATION mode, the latter providing end-to-end consistency checking while retaining swappable labels. In NSFNet timing benchmarks with common per-hop quantum error correction (QEC), SAH eliminates the external-header/payload alignment condition, so timing uncertainty appears as memory residence and delivered latency rather than alignment-budget drops. With a $T_2$-based memory-residence penalty, the advantage depends on router memory coherence; for sufficiently long coherence, SAH maintains higher acceptance and throughput than OBS+QEC and QW+QEC under high jitter.

</details>


### [144] [Geometric Mode Steering of the Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14501)
*Yingying Hong,Longxing Xu,Weiwei Zhang,Jie Ren,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预耗散几何操控协议，通过相干旋转与非选择投影测量相结合，在弛豫开始前重塑量子态，压制与最慢Liouvillian模式的重叠，实现量子Mpemba效应——初始状态距离稳态更远但弛豫更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开量子系统弛豫到稳态的瓶颈常由最慢Liouvillian模式决定，现有策略需选择特殊初态或设计耗散器，本文旨在证明这两者都非必要，探索更普适的加速弛豫方法。

Method: 提出预耗散几何操控协议：在弛豫开始前对任意纯态或混态进行相干旋转与非选择投影测量的交错操作，沿测地线路径操控布洛赫方向，压制与最慢Liouvillian模式的重叠。

Result: 给出了两个可计算条件（减少慢模重叠、更大的初始稳态距离）下实现量子Mpemba效应的判据；统一处理实谱隙和复谱隙情形；在驱动量子比特和多量子比特系统中验证了鲁棒的Mpemba加速。

Conclusion: 所提协议可在固定Lindblad生成器下实现加速弛豫，无需特殊初态或定制耗散器，在离子阱和超导平台中具有实验可行性。

Abstract: The slowest Liouvillian mode often bottlenecks the relaxation of an open quantum system to its steady state. Standard strategies circumvent this bottleneck by selecting special initial states or engineering the dissipator. Here we show that neither is necessary. We introduce a pre-dissipative geometric steering protocol that reshapes any given pure or mixed state before relaxation begins -- coherent rotations interleaved with nonselective projective measurements -- at fixed Lindblad generator. By steering the state's Bloch direction along geodesic paths, the protocol suppresses its overlap with the slowest Liouvillian modes. The prepared state then starts farther from equilibrium yet relaxes faster, realizing the quantum Mpemba effect, whenever two computable conditions hold: reduced slow-mode overlap and a larger initial distance to stationarity. Our framework treats real and complex spectral gaps uniformly, and we demonstrate robust Mpemba acceleration in driven qubit and multiqubit systems using operations available in trapped-ion and superconducting platforms.

</details>


### [145] [Structure-Aware Variational State Preparation for Quantum Basket Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14518)
*Dongwoo Kim,Zhenyu Cui,Daniel K. Park,Chihoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量列车（TT）秩信息的结构感知量子态制备框架，用于量子幅度估计（QAE）的篮子期权定价。该框架利用TT秩信息设计浅层变分态制备电路，在独立情况下移除不必要的纠缠连接，在相关篮子情况下局部制备资产边缘分布并训练紧凑潜变量块以匹配篮子累积分布函数（CDF）。实验表明，该方法将指数级态制备深度缩放替换为线性缩放，同时保持低百分比定价误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 篮子期权定价通常依赖蒙特卡洛估计，量子幅度估计（QAE）可提供二次加速，但QAE的实际收益受限于态制备电路的深度。因此，需要设计更浅的态制备电路以充分发挥QAE的加速优势。

Method: 提出结构感知的量子态制备框架：(1) 在独立资产情形下，利用TT秩信息从硬件高效拟设中移除不必要的纠缠连接；(2) 在相关篮子情形下，局部制备各资产边缘分布，并训练一个紧凑的潜变量块来匹配篮子CDF（篮子推前分布），而非完整联合状态。Basket-CDF目标函数直接对齐态制备与篮子依赖的收益函数。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的电路将精确幅度加载的指数级态制备深度缩放替换为线性缩放，同时保持低百分比的篮子定价误差。额外的基于采样的训练实验和端到端QAE集成研究支持该方法与样本估计训练及标准QAE定价工作流的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该结构感知框架有效降低了QAE篮子期权定价中态制备电路的深度需求，从指数级降至线性级，同时保持定价精度，为量子金融中实际应用QAE加速提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Basket option pricing often relies on Monte Carlo estimation, for which quantum amplitude estimation (QAE) provides a quadratic speed-up. However, the practical benefit of QAE can be limited by the depth of the state-preparation circuit. We propose a structure-aware quantum state-preparation framework for QAE-based basket option pricing. The framework uses tensor-train (TT) rank information to design shallow variational state-preparation circuits. In the independent regime, TT ranks remove unnecessary entangling links from a hardware-efficient ansatz. In correlated basket settings, we instead prepare asset-wise marginals locally and train a compact latent block to match the basket cumulative distribution function. The Basket-CDF objective targets the basket pushforward distribution rather than the full joint state, directly aligning state preparation with basket-dependent payoffs. Numerical experiments show that the proposed circuits replace the exponential state-preparation depth scaling of exact amplitude loading with linear scaling, while maintaining low-percent basket-pricing errors. Additional sampling-based training experiments and an end-to-end QAE integration study support compatibility with sample-estimated training and standard QAE-based pricing workflows.

</details>


### [146] [No Finite NPA Level Characterizes the Complete Quantum Set in the Simplest Bell Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14569)
*Anubhav Chaturvedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在双二分测量（每方两个二元测量）的最小量子场景中，NPA层次结构的任意有限层都无法精确刻画完整量子集，其非量子行为会累积于局部确定性行为处。这一结论通过对称双倾斜CHSH泛函的量子最大值与固定NPA层逼近值的渐近差异分析得出，并利用Motzkin多项式非平方和性质证明有限层误差有界性必然导致矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPA层次结构是量子关联刻画的标准半定松弛方法，但任意有限层是否能精确等于量子集这一基本问题长期未解。解决此问题对理解量子关联的复杂度、量子信息处理的理论基础至关重要。

Method: 构造对称双倾斜CHSH泛函h_α，分析其量子最大值ω_Q的渐近行为；对比固定NPA层近似值ω_L的渐近行为，证明两者差异在T→0时发散；利用边界重标度将算子期望收敛到Motzkin多项式，通过该多项式非平方和性质导出有限层误差有界性矛盾。

Result: 任意有限NPA层都不精确：ω_Q渐近为(4/3)T³，而固定NPA层误差ω_L-ω_Q的阶数高于T³（发散）。所有基于固定有限词表的标准NPA松弛严格包含完整量子集，非量子行为在局部确定性行为处累积。

Conclusion: CHSH及单边倾斜CHSH的有限层精确性不能推广到最小场景的完整量子集刻画，量子关联的半定松弛在有限层级别存在本质局限性。

Abstract: The Navascués--Pironio--Acín (NPA) hierarchy gives the standard semidefinite outer approximations to quantum behaviors. Whether \emph{any} finite level can already equal the quantum set has remained open even in the bipartite scenario with two binary measurements per party. We demonstrate that \emph{no finite level} is exact. For the symmetric doubly tilted CHSH functional $h_α=A_0B_0+A_0B_1+A_1B_0-A_1B_1+α(A_0+B_0)$, set $T=1-α$. Its quantum maximum satisfies $[ω_{\rm Q}(1-T)-(4-2T)]/T^3\to4/3$, whereas every fixed NPA level satisfies $[ω_L(1-T)-ω_{\rm Q}(1-T)]/T^3\to+\infty$. Under the corresponding boundary rescaling, an explicit expectation of the positive operator $ω_{\rm Q}(1-t^2)I-H_t$ converges to the Motzkin polynomial. A bounded fixed-level error would therefore make the Motzkin polynomial plus a nonnegative constant a sum of squares, which is impossible. Consequently, every standard NPA relaxation based on a fixed finite list of words in the measurement projectors strictly contains the complete quantum set, and its nonquantum behaviors accumulate at a local deterministic behavior. Thus, the finite-level exactness of CHSH and all one-sided tilted CHSH maxima does not extend to an exact finite-level description of the complete quantum set in the minimal scenario.

</details>


### [147] [Exact No Signaling in Time without Temporal Classicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14583)
*Minsu Kim,Jeongho Bang,Han Seb Moon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，时间无信号（NSIT）条件并不能保证经典时序过程。通过引入非选择性测量通道的共同不动点（CFP）机制，发现满足精确成对NSIT的态仍可能违反Leggett-Garg不等式，包括最大混合态，从而推翻NSIT作为经典性判据的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为NSIT（时间无信号）是非侵入可测性的干净操作遗迹，满足NSIT就意味着时序过程表现为经典。本文旨在推翻这一错误推断，揭示NSIT与经典性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 引入非选择性测量通道的共同不动点（CFP）作为精确机制；在qutrit环上施加局部简并Lüders测量，解析求解完整CFP流形；通过隐变量重构、熵见证、协议景观扫描、噪声鲁棒性和有限次模拟等多种手段验证。

Result: CFP流形上的每个态都满足精确成对NSIT，但所有非平凡成员（包括最大混合态）都违反Leggett-Garg不等式。违反程度由有限分支位移而非残余信号决定。

Conclusion: 精确NSIT仅证明边际信号在结果擦除后消失，并不保证存在经典时序历史。NSIT不能作为经典性的判据。

Abstract: No signaling in time (NSIT) has often been treated as the clean operational remnant of noninvasive measurability. If an earlier measurement leaves every later marginal unchanged, the temporal process appears classical. We show that this inference is false. We introduce common fixed points (CFPs) of nonselective measurement channels as an exact mechanism that erases all marginal evidence of invasiveness while preserving disturbed outcome conditioned branches. For a qutrit ring subject to a local degenerate Luders measurement, we solve the full CFP manifold analytically. Every state on this manifold satisfies exact pairwise NSIT, yet every nontrivial member violates a Leggett Garg inequality, including the maximally mixed state. The violation is governed by finite branch displacement, not by residual signaling. Hidden variable reconstruction, entropic witnesses, protocol landscape scans, noise robustness, and finite shot simulations show that exact NSIT certifies only the disappearance of marginal signals after outcome erasure, not the existence of a classical temporal history.

</details>


### [148] [Adaptive Entanglement Management in Quantum Multi-Core Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14592)
*Rajeswari Suance P S,Anubhab Dutta,Ruchika Gupta,John Jose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种多核量子处理器的纠缠管理范式：按需生成(ODG)、持续预生成(CGP)和自适应持续预生成(ACGP)。ACGP根据通信模式动态调整纠缠生成概率，显著降低了平均隐形传态延迟，且通过纠缠纯化可有效恢复保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多核量子计算架构中，量子网络芯片(NoC)上的核间通信依赖于纠缠辅助的量子隐形传态，高效的纠缠生成对系统性能至关重要，因此需要研究不同的纠缠管理策略。

Method: 使用扩展的SeQUeNCe模拟器，在基于网格的多核架构上对三种纠缠管理范式（ODG、CGP和提出的ACGP）进行了比较研究，并在真实基准电路上评估性能。

Result: ACGP相比ODG和CGP显著降低了平均隐形传态延迟；预生成虽因存储时间带来保真度退化，但纠缠纯化能以最小延迟代价有效恢复保真度。

Conclusion: 自适应纠缠管理能显著提升可扩展量子多核系统中的通信效率。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computing architectures increasingly rely on multi-core designs, where qubits are distributed across multiple processing cores interconnected through a quantum Network-on-Chip (NoC). In such systems, inter-core communication is typically realized through entanglement-assisted quantum teleportation, making efficient entanglement generation critical for performance. In this paper, we perform a comparative study of three entanglement management paradigms for multi-core quantum processors: reactive on-demand generation (ODG), proactive continuous pre-generation (CGP), and an adaptive continuous pre-generation approach (ACGP). While ODG generates entanglement only when required, CGP reduces average teleportation latency by pre-generating EPR pairs in the background. To improve upon this, we propose ACGP which dynamically adjusts entanglement generation probabilities based on observed inter-core communication patterns. We evaluate these approaches using an extended SeQUeNCe simulator on mesh-based multi-core architectures on real benchmark circuits. Results show that ACGP significantly reduces average teleportation latency compared to ODG and CGP. Although pre-generation introduces fidelity degradation due to storage time, entanglement purification effectively restores fidelity with minimal impact on latency. These results demonstrate that adaptive entanglement managements can substantially improve communication efficiency in scalable quantum multi-core systems.

</details>


### [149] [Worst-Case Quantum Algorithm for Optimal Polynomial Intersection Beyond Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14650)
*Shuji Horinaga,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对最优多项式交集（OPI）问题，提出了一种新的量子算法，在worst-case下超越了DQI算法的参数范围；同时改进了Sun和Wootters的存在性界，并将结果推广到Max-LINSAT问题和MDS码场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sun和Wootters证明了即使在最坏情况下，OPI在更大参数范围内也存在解，但未给出量子算法。本文旨在设计一种在worst-case下超越DQI参数范围的量子算法，并进一步改进存在性界。

Method: 通过将Brascamp-Lieb型不等式创新性地应用于MDS码场景，结合MDS码对偶的高效列表解码器，设计量子算法并推导存在性界。

Result: 1) 当每个子集包含约一半域元素时，算法在R>0.75时达到满意率s=1，匹配平均情况界，而DQI需R=1才能达到s=1；2) 存在性界从R>0.7495改进至R>0.7158；3) 结果推广至Max-LINSAT问题和一般MDS码。

Conclusion: 本文给出了首個在worst-case下超越DQI参数范围的量子算法，改进了存在性界，并将方法推广到更一般的编码理论问题，Brascamp-Lieb型不等式有望进一步应用。

Abstract: The Optimal Polynomial Intersection (OPI) problem asks us to find a low-degree polynomial over a finite field whose values lie in prescribed subsets on as many given inputs as possible. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) gives a quantum algorithm for OPI in parameter regimes beyond those achieved by the best known classical heuristics. Follow-up works improve the parameter regimes, but their analyses are limited to average-case settings. Recently, Sun and Wootters showed that, even in the worst case, OPI has a solution in a larger parameter regime than the one covered by DQI. However, they left open whether one can design a quantum algorithm that solves OPI in the worst-case beyond the DQI regime.
  We give such a quantum algorithm. As a byproduct, we also improve the existential bound of Sun and Wootters in certain parameter regimes. In particular, when each subset contains roughly half of the field elements, our algorithm finds a solution with satisfaction rate $s=1$ whenever the rate satisfies $R>0.75$. This matches the previous average-case bound, whereas DQI cannot achieve $s=1$ unless $R=1$. Our existential bound guarantees the existence of a solution when $R> 0.7158$, improving over the previous threshold $R>0.7495$. More generally, our existential results extend to the Max-LINSAT problem with respect to arbitrary maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. The corresponding algorithmic results apply only to MDS codes whose dual admits an efficient list decoder. Our results are obtained through a novel application of a Brascamp--Lieb-type inequality in the MDS setting, which may have further applications.

</details>


### [150] [High-rate continuous-variable quantum key distribution coexisting with Tb/s coherent classical transmission in hollow-core fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14704)
*Xitao Ji,Siyu Chen,Peng Li,Mingming Zhang,Yilun Chen,Jun Gao,Rui Lin,Bacco Davide,Siqi Yan,Ming Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超低损耗反谐振空芯光纤结合残余载波辅助的离散调制连续变量量子密钥分发（DM-CV-QKD），在24.3公里链路上实现了153.22 Mb/s的渐近密钥率和149.99 Mb/s的有限尺寸密钥率，同时与39个相干波分复用通道（总数据速率7.6 Tb/s）共存，展示了空芯光纤在集成高容量量子安全相干光网络中的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固态芯光纤中，量子密钥分发与高容量经典光通信共存时，弱量子态受传播诱导的过量噪声限制，导致QKD性能严重受限。为此，本文旨在通过空芯光纤和载波辅助相位恢复技术解决共存噪声和低信噪比相位恢复两大关键问题。

Method: 采用超低损耗反谐振空芯光纤作为传输介质，使用残余载波辅助的离散调制连续变量QKD（DM-CV-QKD）方案，在24.3公里链路上与39个相干WDM通道共存，进行实验验证和有限尺寸密钥分析。

Result: 在24.3公里、3.3 dB端到端损耗的空芯光纤链路上，15-Gbaud DM-CV-QKD通道获得平均渐近SKR 153.22 Mb/s和有限尺寸SKR 149.99 Mb/s；39个WDM通道实现总数据速率7.6 Tb/s、净速率7.2 Tb/s；无需光带通滤波器即可在高达15 dBm经典发射功率下维持正密钥率；100公里等效条件下有限尺寸分析仍能获得正密钥率。

Conclusion: 反谐振空芯光纤结合载波辅助相位恢复可显著扩展共享光纤量子安全相干链路的运行区间，为将高码率CV-QKD集成到高容量光网络提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) can provide secret keys with security rooted in quantum mechanics, but operation alongside high-capacity classical traffic remains limited by the excess-noise budget of weak quantum states in conventional solid-core fiber. Here, we combine ultralow-loss anti-resonant hollow-core fiber with residual-carrier-assisted discrete-modulation continuous-variable QKD (DM-CV-QKD) to address both propagation-induced coexistence noise and low-SNR phase recovery. Over a 24.3-km hollow-core link with 3.3-dB end-to-end loss, a dual-polarization 15-Gbaud DM-CV-QKD channel achieves an average asymptotic secret-key rate (SKR) of 153.22 Mb/s and a finite-size SKR of 149.99 Mb/s, while 39 coherent wavelength-division-multiplexed channels deliver an aggregate data rate of 7.6 Tb/s and a net data rate of 7.2 Tb/s. The system can even sustain a positive SKR under a high classical launch power of up to 15 dBm, without an optical bandpass filter (BPF). Finite-size analysis against collective attacks further yields a projected positive secret-key rate at a 100-km-equivalent condition. These results show that an anti-resonant hollow-core fiber, combined with carrier-assisted phase recovery, can greatly extend the operating regime of shared-fiber quantum-secured coherent links, pointing to a promising approach for integrating high-rate CV-QKD with high-capacity optical networks.

</details>


### [151] [Moment Optimization in the Navascués-Pironio-Acín Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14755)
*Francesco Flora,Losel Matos,Tim Heightman,Tamás Kriváchy,Adan Garriga,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非交换多项式优化中NPA层级SDP松弛的矩选择问题，将矩选择重构为组合子集选择，利用复杂系统理论中的边际协同诊断量化矩间的高阶协同交互，提出了并行退火、基于RBM的强化学习和贝叶斯优化三种方法。在$I_{3322}$ Bell不等式基准上，RBM方法接近最优且成本低于暴力搜索两个数量级；将该框架应用于$(4,4,2,2)$场景的174个Bell不等式和一维海森堡自旋链，证明物理基可压缩且非全局最优，预算感知搜索将长程相关界限提升近两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPA层级中算子矩数量随层级组合增长，实际计算受限于预算；并非所有矩对边界紧致性贡献相同，因此在固定计算预算下选择最优矩子集是一个关键问题。

Method: 将矩选择建模为组合子集选择问题，引入复杂系统理论的边际协同诊断量化高阶交互；提出并行退火(PT)、基于RBM的强化学习策略和贝叶斯优化(BO)三种方法进行优化。

Result: 三种方法在$I_{3322}$基准上均显著优于贪心方法，成本低于暴力搜索约两个数量级；RBM在硬过渡区域最接近最优。在174个Bell不等式测试中发现异构收敛行为，在自旋链中证明物理单式基可压缩且非全局最优，预算感知搜索将长程相关界限提升近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 建立了可扩展的矩选择框架，广泛应用于量子物理和量子信息中的非交换多项式优化问题。

Abstract: The Navascués-Pironio-Acín (NPA) hierarchy provides a convergent sequence of semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations for noncommutative polynomial optimisation, ubiquitous in quantum physics. However, its practical applicability is limited by the combinatorial growth in operator moments required at each level. Since not all moments contribute equally to bound tightness, selecting moments within a fixed computational budget is a relevant problem.
  We reframe moment selection as combinatorial subset selection and show it is governed by strong higher-order synergistic interactions among moments, quantified through a marginal synergy diagnostic adapted from complex systems theory. We develop and compare three optimisation methods: Parallel Tempering (PT), an RBM-based reinforcement learning policy, and Bayesian Optimisation (BO). On the $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality benchmark, all three substantially outperform greedy approaches at costs around two orders of magnitude below brute force, with the RBM achieving the closest approach to optimal throughout the hard transition regime.
  We apply the framework to the 174 Bell inequalities in the $(4,4,2,2)$ scenario, finding heterogeneous convergence behaviour across inequalities, and to the one-dimensional Heisenberg spin chain, demonstrating that physically motivated monomial bases are internally compressible and are not globally optimal in general. A budget-aware search over a broader pool improves certified bounds on long-range correlations by nearly two orders of magnitude. These results establish a scalable framework for moment selection in noncommutative polynomial optimisation, with broad applications across quantum physics and quantum information.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum Remote Implementation of Hybrid Operations on Hyperstates Using Hyperentangled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14819)
*Satish Kumar,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于偏振和空间双自由度超纠缠态的量子远程操作实现(QRIO)协议，利用线性光学元件和交叉Kerr非线性相互作用，实现对未知单光子双量子比特超态的任意混合算子远程操控，并分析了测量误差对成功概率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRIO协议大多采用超纠缠态但每次仅利用单一自由度，限制了量子资源的利用效率。本文旨在同时利用偏振和空间两个自由度，实现对任意混合算子的远程实现，提升量子远程操控的能力。

Method: 采用两量子比特超纠缠态(偏振+空间模式)作为共享资源，利用线性光学元件和交叉Kerr非线性相互作用实现光子-光子耦合，通过同时操控两个自由度来实现任意混合算子的远程执行。

Result: 分析了有限相干态可区分性和相干态耗散导致的测量误差对协议的影响，评估了协议的成功概率。结果表明，适当选择交叉Kerr相移和相干态振幅可显著提升协议性能。

Conclusion: 该协议为混合量子通信和分布式量子信息处理提供了有前景的方案，通过双自由度同时利用提高了量子远程操控的效率和灵活性。

Abstract: Quantum remote control, also known as quantum remote implementation of an operator (QRIO), enables the remote manipulation of an arbitrary quantum state by implementing a desired quantum operation at a distant location. Significant progress has recently been made in developing QRIO protocols and their variants. Most existing schemes employ hyperentangled states where entanglement is shared across multiple degrees of freedom (DOFs). However, these protocols typically exploit only one degree of freedom at a time. In this work, we propose a QRIO protocol that simultaneously utilizes the polarization and spatial DOFs of a two-qubit hyperentangled state to remotely implement an arbitrary hybrid operator on an unknown single-photon two-qubit hyperstate. The shared hyperentangled resource is realized using the polarization and spatial modes of photons, while the protocol is constructed using linear optical elements and cross-Kerr nonlinear interactions to facilitate effective photon-photon coupling.
